*Stephen McIntosh,Reuben Smit,Daisuke Saito,Nobuaki Minematsu,Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自监督WavLM表征和DTW的无文本框架，用于评估L2语音的发音准确性、节奏和语调，在英语和日语上表现良好，部分任务接近人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L2语音评估侧重音段特征，超音段特征（节奏、语调）评分未被充分探索；且现有方法依赖标注L2数据，难以应用于低资源场景。

Method: 使用自监督WavLM表征和DTW（动态时间规整）进行无文本L2语音评估：发音准确性通过DTW距离比较学习者与母语模板；节奏通过DTW对齐路径的扭曲程度度量；语调结合DTW距离与韵律残差、音高和强度特征。

Result: DTW方法在整体和句子级发音评分上超越人类一致性；节奏评估方法接近人类水平；语调评估在某些任务上表现仍较有限。

Conclusion: 自监督表征（如WavLM）结合DTW为多维度无文本发音评估提供了有前景的基础，尤其适合低资源场景。

Abstract: L2 speech assessment has traditionally focused on phonetic assessment, leaving the scoring of suprasegmental features such as rhythm and intonation underexplored. Moreover, assessment methods often require training with labeled L2 speech data, making them difficult to apply in low-resource settings. We investigate whether DTW over self-supervised WavLM representations can provide a text-free framework for assessing phonetic accuracy, rhythm, and intonation in English and Japanese L2 speech. Results show that a basic DTW-based approach that compares learner speech to native templates exceeds human agreement on holistic and sentence-level phonetic scoring. For rhythm, we introduce methods that measure the degree of warping in the DTW alignment path; our best method approaches human-level performance. For intonation, we combine DTW distance over prosodic residuals with pitch and intensity features, but performance remains more modest on some tasks. Our results point to self-supervised representations as a promising, text-free basis for multi-aspect pronunciation assessment.

</details>


### [29] [Post-Training Shifts Confidence: A Three-Stage Analysis of How SFT, RL, and OPD Shape Pre-, Intra-, and Post-CoT Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13753)
*Shuhao Li,Guodong Du,Anhao Zhao,Wanyu Lin,Tianyu Yuan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段校准框架，系统评估监督微调、强化学习和在策略蒸馏三种后训练方法在推理过程中的置信度表现，发现置信度可靠性具有位置依赖性，并据此提出位置感知置信策略PosConf，显著提升答案聚合和早期停止的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅用最终答案准确性评估后训练方法，忽略了这些方法如何重塑推理过程中的置信度分布，缺乏对置信度校准的深入理解。

Method: 构建三阶段校准框架（推理前困难估计、推理中早期终止、推理后答案聚合），在数学推理基准上对比SFT、RL和OPD三种后训练方法的置信度表现，并提出位置感知置信策略PosConf。

Result: OPD提供最有效的推理前置信度，SFT提供最强的在线早期停止信号，RL产生最可靠的推理后聚合信号；置信度可靠性具有位置依赖性，PosConf在RL答案聚合上比多数投票提升6.1个点，在OPD早期停止上提升4.3个点。

Conclusion: 推理模型中的置信度应分阶段且位置感知地使用。

Abstract: Large language models have made strong reasoning gains through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation, yet these post-training methods are usually evaluated only by final-answer accuracy. We study how they reshape confidence during reasoning. We introduce a three-stage calibration framework that evaluates confidence before, during, and after chain-of-thought generation, corresponding to difficulty estimation, early termination, and answer aggregation. Through a controlled comparison on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we find that OPD provides the most useful pre-reasoning confidence, SFT gives the strongest online signal for early stopping, and RL produces the most reliable trace-level signal for aggregation. We further show that confidence reliability is position-dependent: RL confidence becomes informative after a path-commitment phase, while OPD confidence is useful early but can become inversely calibrated later. Based on this observation, we propose PosConf, a position-aware confidence strategy that uses confidence only from reliable relative-position intervals. PosConf improves RL answer aggregation by 6.1 points over majority voting and consistently improves OPD early stopping under tight token budgets, with gains up to 4.3 points by avoiding its later inverse-calibration region, showing that \emph{confidence in reasoning models should be used both stage-wise and position-awarely}. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Post-Training-Calibration.

</details>


### [30] [SPyCE: Skill-Policy Co-evolution for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13854)
*Ru Zhang,Weijie Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPyCE框架，通过技能-策略共同进化机制，将多模态推理轨迹蒸馏为层次化技能库，在强化学习过程中协同优化技能库和策略模型，显著优于现有的RL和记忆基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法将轨迹简化为标量奖励，迫使策略从零开始发现可复用的工具使用模式；记忆方法依赖测试时检索而不更新策略。多模态推理轨迹应被蒸馏为可复用技能，与策略共同进化。

Method: 提出SPyCE框架：(1) 构建层次化技能库，包含执行技能（局部视觉操作）和工作流技能（高层次工具编排先验）；(2) 在RL训练过程中，策略模型基于检索到的技能指导轨迹生成，同时技能库利用策略产出的有价值轨迹不断进化，形成闭环。

Result: 在八个基准上，SPyCE一致优于基于RL和基于记忆的基线方法。层次化技能设计和共同进化机制对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 技能-策略联合优化是构建强大多模态智能体的有前景范式。

Abstract: Multimodal agents that think with images iteratively manipulate visual evidence and invoke tools across many steps. Existing reinforcement learning methods reduce trajectories to scalar rewards, forcing the policy to discover reusable tool-use patterns from scratch on every new task; memory-based alternatives retain past experience, yet they rely on test-time retrieval, without updating the policy to absorb reusable patterns from that experience. Our key insight is that multimodal reasoning trajectories should be distilled into reusable skills that co-evolve with the policy during training, rather than being consumed as rewards or retrieved from a static store. To this end, we propose SPyCE (Skill-Policy Co-evolution), a framework that distills trajectories into a hierarchical skill library and updates it throughout reinforcement learning. Execution skills capture local visual operations, while workflow skills encode high-level priors that orchestrate tool use. During training, the policy model conditions on retrieved skills to guide its rollouts, while the skill library evolves using valuable rollouts generated by the policy. This creates a closed loop in which improved policies yield better skills, and the evolving skill library, in turn, provides stronger priors for policy rollouts. Experiments across eight benchmarks demonstrate that SPyCE consistently outperforms both RL-based and memory-based baselines. Further analysis reveals that both the hierarchical skill design and the co-evolution mechanism are critical to our design. These results suggest joint skill-policy optimization as a promising paradigm for building capable multimodal agents.

</details>


### [31] [High-Order Question Generation in a Multilingual Educational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13901)
*Suna-Şeyma Uçar,Itziar Aldabe,Nora Aranberri,Orphée De Clercq*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了用基于Claim-Evidence-Reasoning和Divergent Questioning两种替代框架的提示词，在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言中生成高阶问题。结果表明，开源和专有模型均能有效生成三种语言的问题，但仅约50%的可回答问题被教师认定为高阶问题。替代框架能产生结构性和概念性多样的问题，可相互补充并作为Bloom分类法的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度依赖Bloom分类法且仅聚焦英语，缺乏对其他框架和多语言环境下的高阶问题生成的探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入Claim-Evidence-Reasoning和Divergent Questioning两种框架的提示词，在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言环境下，使用开源和专有LLM生成问题，并由教师评估问题质量。

Result: 两种模型均能有效生成三种语言的问题；但仅约一半的可回答问题被教师认定为高阶问题；替代框架生成的问题在结构和概念上具有多样性。

Conclusion: 替代框架可相互补充，为Bloom分类法提供可行的替代方案，扩展了多语言高阶问题生成的研究视角。

Abstract: Critical thinking is a fundamental skill that helps learners move beyond simple memorization. One way to develop this skill is through high-order questioning. However, crafting such questions remains a challenge for educators, and classroom practices tend to rely on low-order questions. Large Language Models have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating high-order questions, especially when guided by prompts based on Bloom's Taxonomy. Yet, existing research has largely centered on this framework and focused only on English. This study addresses these gaps by introducing prompts grounded in two alternative frameworks: Claim-Evidence-Reasoning and Divergent Questioning within a multilingual context using Basque, Spanish, and English. Results indicate that while both an open-source and a proprietary model rather effectively generate questions in all three languages, only about half of the answerable questions are recognized by teachers as high-order. A positive finding is that the alternative frameworks produce structurally and conceptually varied questions, suggesting they could complement each other and provide viable alternatives to Bloom's Taxonomy.

</details>


### [32] [DeepStress: Stress-Testing Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13920)
*Ismael Rousseau,Geraldine Damnati,Frederic Bechet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了DeepStress压力测试框架，通过控制检索证据的可信度、相关性和事实性三个维度，评估搜索智能体在处理低质量证据时的鲁棒性，并在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus数据集上测试了多个智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体在多步问答中表现优异，但它们在面对低质量证据时的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。现实基准测试中低质量证据很少出现，但在实际应用中可能导致灾难性失败。

Method: 提出DeepStress压力测试框架，用受控合成环境替换搜索智能体的检索模块，控制影响文档可靠性的三个维度：可信度、相关性和事实性。

Result: 在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus数据集上的测试表明，不同智能体在处理不可靠信息的能力上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 提出了能更好记录系统输出以及参数化知识与检索知识之间冲突的新评估指标。

Abstract: While search agents demonstrate impressive capabilities in multi-step question answering, their robustness to poor-quality evidence remains under-explored. This phenomenon occurs rarely in realistic benchmarks but can lead to dramatic failure in real life applications. Therefore in this study we propose DeepStress, a stress testing framework that controls the frequency of challenging evidence by replacing the retrieval module of search agents with a controlled synthetic environment. We use this framework to control three dimensions that can affect document reliability: trustworthiness, relevance, and factuality. Testing several search agents on HotpotQA and BrowseCompPlus, we demonstrate that agents exhibit substantial differences in their ability to handle unreliable information and propose new metrics that better document systems outcomes as well as the interactions between conflicting parametric and retrieved knowledge.

</details>


### [33] [DeltaMerge-LowRes: Composing Language and Task Deltas for Low-Resource Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13967)
*Son Ha Xuan,Xuan-Bach Le,Phat T. Tran-Truong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ΔMergeLowRes 方法，将多语言编码器适配新语言和新任务解耦为独立的语言增量 (Δ_L) 和任务增量 (Δ_T)，并通过四种权重组合规则（加法、激活引导、稀疏感知、新提出的跨轴 TIES）在推理时组合。在4个任务族和4种非洲语言上的实验表明，跨轴 TIES 在摘要任务上表现最佳（chrF提升+4到+7），在QA任务上F1提升+2.32、EM提升+2.91，稀疏感知合并可将分类任务的ECE降低36%且宏F1不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源场景下，传统的语言-任务联合微调成本高昂。本文探索能否将语言适应和任务适应解耦为两个独立的增量，并在权重空间中通过组合规则高效融合，从而避免昂贵的联合微调。

Method: 提出 ΔMergeLowRes 框架：从无标注单语文本学习语言增量 Δ_L，从英文标注数据学习任务增量 Δ_T；推理时通过四种规则（加法、激活引导、稀疏感知、跨轴 TIES）组合二者。跨轴 TIES 将 TIES-Merging 的修剪、符号选举和合并步骤从两个任务轴扩展到语言轴和任务轴。

Result: 在4个任务族（摘要、QA、分类等）和4种非洲语言的158个实验单元上，跨轴 TIES 在摘要任务上3/4语言chrF提升+4到+7（18.59 vs. 13.80），QA任务F1+2.32、EM+2.91；稀疏感知合并使分类ECE降低36%且宏F1不变。组合规则显著影响模型保留、抑制和校准的特性。

Conclusion: 语言增量和任务增量可以在权重空间中解耦学习并有效组合，无需昂贵的联合微调。组合规则的选择对最终模型性能有实质性影响，跨轴 TIES 和稀疏感知合并各自在不同任务上表现出优势。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Adapting a multilingual encoder to a new language \emph{and} a new task with only a few hundred gold examples is a common low-resource NLP setting, yet the two axes are usually fused via an expensive language--task fine-tuning run. We ask whether they can instead be trained separately and recombined in weight space. \DeltaMergeLowRes{} learns a language delta $Δ_L$ from unlabeled monolingual text and a task delta $Δ_T$ from labeled English data, then composes them at inference under one of four rules: additive, activation-guided, sparsity-aware, and a novel \emph{cross-axis TIES}. The new rule adapts the TIES-Merging steps of trimming, sign election, and merging to the language and task axes rather than to two task axes. Holding $(Δ_L,Δ_T)$ fixed across rules on four task families and four African languages ($158$ evaluated cells, $10{,}000$-sample paired bootstrap per cell), we find: (i) cross-axis TIES wins summarisation on $3/4$ languages by $+4$ to $+7$ chrF (chrF $18.59$ vs.\ $13.80$ task-only); (ii) it improves QA F1 by $+2.32$ and EM by $+2.91$; and (iii) sparsity-aware merging cuts classification ECE by $36\%$ at parity macro-F1. The composition rule materially changes what the merged model preserves, suppresses, and calibrates. We release all JSON traces and a claim ledger.

</details>


### [34] [Constraint-Aware Counterfactual Editing for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13977)
*S M Rafiuddin,Vamsi Krishna Pavuluri,Atriya Sen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAVE-ABSA框架，通过约束感知的验证编辑方法生成和验证面向方面的反事实样本，确保仅翻转目标方面的情感极性，同时保持非目标方面情感、语义、流畅性和事实一致性不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实生成方法通常关注句子级别的标签翻转，产生的编辑虽然流畅但可能在方面级别无效、语义漂移或矛盾，缺乏对方面级情感反事实的有效验证机制。

Method: CAVE-ABSA框架包含五个步骤：定位目标方面的意见跨度、执行受控反事实改写、通过修复模块优化候选、利用方面级验证（语义相似度、AMR结构保留、编辑最小性、流畅性、矛盾检测）进行过滤。

Result: 能够生成在方面级别有效、语义一致、结构保持的反事实样本，可用于ABSA模型的鲁棒性评估和数据增强。

Conclusion: 通过将生成与验证明确分离，CAVE-ABSA提供了一种生成有意义的面向局部的反事实的原则性方法，用于测试ABSA模型是否真正依赖基于方面的情感推理。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) requires models to identify sentiment toward specific aspects rather than relying on the global polarity of a sentence. This makes counterfactual evaluation especially challenging: a valid counterfactual should flip the sentiment of one target aspect while preserving the sentiment of all non-target aspects, semantic meaning, fluency, and factual consistency. Existing counterfactual generation methods often focus on sentence-level label flipping and may produce edits that are fluent but aspect-invalid, semantically drifting, or contradictory. To address this limitation, we propose CAVE-ABSA, a Constraint-Aware Validated Editing framework for generating and validating aspect-level counterfactuals. CAVE-ABSA localizes the opinion span associated with the target aspect, performs controlled counterfactual rewriting, refines candidates through a repair module, and filters them using aspect-level verification, semantic similarity, AMR-guided structural preservation, edit minimality, fluency, and contradiction detection. The framework is designed to construct validated counterfactual ABSA datasets for robustness evaluation and data augmentation. By explicitly separating generation from validation, CAVE-ABSA provides a principled approach for producing meaningful aspect-local counterfactuals and for testing whether ABSA models truly rely on aspect-grounded sentiment reasoning.

</details>


### [35] [Can an Old Dog Be Taught New Tricks? Taking LLMs Beyond Sentence Level Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14040)
*Alaina Brandt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAT系统，一种基于RAG的整文档翻译系统，通过检索可比语料库中的段落、章节和文档级示例，引导LLM进行语用重构翻译。实验表明，LLM可以被引导偏离逐句翻译范式，但重构效果仍不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动翻译系统（从CAT工具到机器翻译）普遍将翻译视为逐句行为，忽略了语篇层面的语用、修辞和话语组织差异。本文旨在探索能否让LLM超越逐句范式，实现整文档级别的、语料库驱动的语用重构翻译。

Method: 构建PAT系统：用户配置规格说明 + 可比语料库（美国英语与拉美西班牙语长篇真实文本）的RAG检索 → 将段落、章节、文档级别的示例传递给LLM进行整文档生成。使用定制化的MQM类型学，由两位经过培训的评估者对三组生成式AI论文的六种自动翻译进行评估（美国英语→拉美/墨西哥西班牙语）。

Result: 有限的prompt设置未产生有意义的语用重构；规格说明和语料库信息提示的翻译有时表现出显著重构，但并非总是有效。LLM可以被引导向重构方向偏离逐句范式，但重构效果仍需改进。

Conclusion: LLM有潜力超越逐句翻译范式实现语用重构，但在自动翻译系统设计、语料库构建和翻译质量评估方法上仍有大量工作要做。

Abstract: Automatic translation systems, from CAT tools to MT, overwhelmingly treat translation as a sentence-by-sentence act. This paper asks whether LLMs can be moved beyond that paradigm through whole-document, corpus-informed translation. We present PAT (Pragmatic Auto-Translator), a RAG-based system that pairs user-configured specifications with context from a comparable corpus of authentic longform texts in U.S. English and Latin American Spanish, passing retrieved paragraph-, section-, and document-level examples to an LLM for whole-document generation. The goal is draft translation for professional verification: target texts reformulated to fit their Spanish-language context, where discourse organization, rhetorical style, and pragmatic norms differ meaningfully from English. We evaluated six automatic translations of essays on generative AI across three projects using a customized MQM typology, assessed by two trained evaluators working from U.S. English into LATAM and Mexican Spanish. Results show that a limited prompt produced no meaningful reformulation, and specifications and corpus-informed translations at times showed substantial reformulation, though not always to effect. We find that LLMs can be moved toward reformulation and away from the sentence-by-sentence paradigm, though more work is needed to improve the effectiveness of those reformulations. In this paper, we discuss considerations related to automatic translation system design, corpus construction, and translation quality evaluation methodology and results.

</details>


### [36] [Hindcast: Replaying Prediction Markets to Evaluate LLM Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14051)
*Xiao Ye,Jacob Dineen,Evan Zhu,Shijie Lu,Kevin Song,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hindcast评估框架，通过冻结Reddit数据快照并设置时间截止点，堵住了LLM回溯测试中因训练数据包含未来信息或检索到事后报告而产生的信息泄露，从而真正评估模型的预测能力而非记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM回溯测试存在严重的信息泄露问题：一是检索模型可以获取事后撰写的报告，将预测任务简化为查询；二是新模型训练数据更接近事件发生时间，导致原本未来的问题变成了训练数据中的记忆。因此需要一种能真正评估模型预测能力而非记忆能力的无泄露评估方法。

Method: Hindcast方法：以选定过去时间点t₀为截止点，冻结公开Reddit快照作为模型唯一可读取的信息源，模型只能阅读t₀之前的帖子；重放已解决的Polymarket预测市场；评分时同时对比真实结果和市场在t₀时刻的自身价格（即人类基于相同历史信息的预测）；截止点按每个市场单独设置，快照永不改变，因此随着模型改进可在新市场上重复评估而不失效。

Result: 堵住信息泄露后，检索对大多数模型仍有帮助，但仅限于Reddit在事件发生前已有相关讨论的情况；如果档案中只有推测性内容，检索反而会损害模型性能。

Conclusion: Hindcast通过严格的时间隔离和信息源限制，有效消除了LLM预测评估中的信息泄露，揭示了检索辅助的真实价值取决于信息来源中是否存在事前讨论而非事后报道。

Abstract: Forecasters are evaluated by backtesting, which replays resolved questions and grades the probability the system would have assigned before the outcome was known. For LLMs, two channels leak the answer into this test. A model that retrieves can surface reports written after the event, turning forecasting into a lookup, and each new model is trained on data closer to the event, so a question that lay in the future for last year's models sits inside this year's training data. Either way, the test grades recall while claiming to grade foresight. We introduce Hindcast, which closes both leaks by grading a model as if it stood at a chosen past date $t_0$, before the outcome existed in either channel. Hindcast replays resolved Polymarket prediction markets against a frozen snapshot of public Reddit, lets the model read only posts written before $t_0$, and scores each forecast against both what happened and the market's own price at $t_0$, itself a human forecast made from the same past information. Because the cutoff is set per market and the snapshot never changes, the evaluation re-runs on new markets as models improve, without going stale. Once the leak is closed, retrieval still helps most models, but only where Reddit discussed the event beforehand. Where the archive carried only speculation, retrieval hurts.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [37] [The Entanglement Wall: Activation-Space Probes as Risk Detectors, Not Context Adjudicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13075)
*Dominik Schwarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究残差流探针能否区分有害请求与表面匹配的良性控制请求。实验显示，激活传感器在分类法选择的集合中阻断了95.5-97.7%的合规攻击，但在跨语境泛化时表现较弱，表明这些激活分数更适合作为广义风险检测器而非独立的上下文判断器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索残差流探针是否能在不改变话题或表面形式的情况下，有效区分有害请求与表面匹配的良性控制请求，即判断上下文是否会改变请求的危害性。

Method: 在三个7-8B模型家族上，使用激活传感器对残差流进行探针分析。采用分类法选择集合、XSTest提示、Twin-n70和Twin-n163子集进行测试，并实施了泄漏、保留和排列控制。同时在参考家族上测试了十个轴，在所有家族上测试了七个轴。

Result: 激活传感器在分类法选择集合中阻断95.5-97.7%的合规攻击，XSTest提示阻断59.6-68.4%。完全独立的审计重建了近上限的源对比AUROC（0.996-0.999），但固定迁移到匹配对时较弱（Twin-n70: 0.656-0.819, Twin-n163: 0.590-0.690）。对训练的分类器在类别和生成批次保留下减弱，在95%语料内TPR下错误阻断79.6-100%的XSTest。

Conclusion: 在测试的读取点上，这些激活分数表现为广义风险检测器而非独立的上下文判断器，无法可靠地作为上下文危害性的独立裁决工具。

Abstract: Context can change whether a request is harmful without changing its topic or surface form. We ask whether residual-stream probes distinguish harmful requests from surface-matched benign controls at a useful operating point. Across three 7-8B model families, an activation sensor blocks 95.5-97.7 percent of judge-classified compliant attacks in a taxonomy-selected set. It also blocks 59.6-68.4 percent of XSTest prompts. A fully disjoint audit reconstructs near-ceiling source-contrast AUROC (0.996-0.999), but fixed transfer to matched pairs is weaker: 0.656-0.819 on the guard-selected Twin-n70 subset and 0.590-0.690 on the full Twin-n163 cohort. We test ten axes on the reference family and seven across all families with leakage, hold-out, and permutation controls. On Twin-n163, no axis evaluated without direct pair-boundary fitting reaches the specified numerical threshold. Requiring persistence on that full cohort was added at analysis time. A separately specified 24B/32B extension gives the same result. Pair-trained classifiers weaken under category and generation-batch hold-out and false-block 79.6-100 percent of XSTest at 95 percent in-corpus TPR. At the tested read points, these activation scores behave as broad-risk detectors rather than standalone context adjudicators.

</details>


### [38] [Operational Evidence Gaps for LLMs in Fraud Detection and Trust-and-Safety Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13078)
*Keyur Gabani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过欺诈优先的视角调查了LLM在信任与安全工作流中的部署证据，发现欺诈领域的部署证据（延迟、成本、校准等）严重不足，而内容审核领域有更公开的证据。论文提出了FORTE组织框架和最低部署证据清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多将LLM作为独立模型评估，较少关注其作为运营管道组件的实际行为。这产生了实践性问题：什么条件能证明将LLM放入具有延迟、成本、升级、人工审查和对抗风险约束的实时工作流中是合理的？

Method: 对49篇操作相关文献进行编码（欺诈检测14篇、内容审核18篇、跨领域17篇），补充15篇上下文参考文献，采用欺诈优先的调查方法分析部署证据。

Result: 存在证据不平衡：欺诈领域是最大的任务特定编码来源，但18篇欺诈和调查来源中，没有报告每次决策的延迟、每次决策的美元成本或校准证据，多数仅报告离线任务性能、检索增益或案例研究准确率。内容审核论文则包含更多关于延迟、成本、治理和公平性的公开证据。

Conclusion: 贡献了FORTE角色与证据组织框架（将LLM定位为分类器、检索接口、解释生成器、审核助手、智能体、特征提取器或升级组件），以及最低部署证据清单（延迟预算、每次决策成本、决策阈值、解释完整性、对抗压力），并提出了支持LLM在欺诈与信任安全工作中部署声明所需的研究议程。

Abstract: LLMs are now proposed for fraud detection, scam investigation, content moderation, and other trust-and-safety workflows. Much of the public literature still evaluates them as models, with less attention to their behavior as components in operational pipelines. This creates a practical evidence question: what would justify placing an LLM inside a live workflow with latency, cost, escalation, human-review, and adversarial-risk constraints?
  We address this question through a fraud-first survey of deployment evidence. We code 49 operationally relevant sources on LLM use in fraud detection, investigation support, content moderation, and cross-cutting robustness (18 fraud, 14 moderation, 17 cross-cutting), supplemented by 15 contextual references that establish the survey boundaries. These sources include systems, benchmarks, frameworks, and deployment-relevant surveys, not 49 production deployments.
  The main finding is an evidence imbalance. Fraud supplies the largest task-specific portion of the coded corpus. The moderation papers, however, include more explicit public evidence on latency, cost, governance, and fairness. Among the 18 fraud and investigation sources, none report clean per-decision latency, per-decision dollar cost, or calibration evidence; most report offline task performance, retrieval gains, or case-study accuracy instead.
  The survey contributes a role-and-evidence organizing frame, FORTE, for locating LLMs as classifiers, retrieval interfaces, explanation generators, reviewer assistants, agents, feature extractors, or escalation components. It also contributes a minimum deployment-evidence checklist covering latency budget, cost per decision, decision threshold, explanation integrity, and adversarial pressure. The resulting agenda identifies studies needed to support deployment claims for LLM-based fraud and trust-and-safety work.

</details>

*SingGuard Team*

TL;DR: nsfaguard是一个面向智能体AI系统的安全护栏框架，基于NSFA分类体系（涵盖185种风险变体）和93K+样本的多语言基准，采用SFT生成式推理与判别式分类头相结合的双模式方法，实现了可解释的离线审计与实时检测（约50ms），在0.8B至9B四个模型上均达到≥94% F1，超越最强竞品6-12个绝对百分点。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发布0.8B、2B、4B、9B四个模型，在目的构建的基准上均达到≥94% F1，超越最强竞品6-12个绝对百分点。9B模型在跨源评估上达到91.29% F1且精度-召回更均衡。消融实验表明分类头可使护栏获得超出原始范围的风险检测能力并达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 该方法具可扩展性和通用性，可作为插件式增强手段提升AI系统安全防护能力。

Abstract: We present nsfaguard, a guardrail framework for securing agentic AI systems against operational threats, such as prompt injection, sensitive information extraction, malicious code requests, dangerous tool misuse, and resource exhaustion. We first introduce the NSFA taxonomy, which organizes 185 risk variants into a CIA-triad-grounded hierarchy and is cross-validated against three well-established OWASP guidelines. Based on this taxonomy, we construct a benchmark suite spanning 133 languages, comprising over 93K purpose-built samples targeting both user queries and agent responses, along with 3,435 cross-source samples adapted from five public agent-security datasets. To detect these operational threats in practice, we develop a dual-mode approach combining SFT-based generative reasoning for interpretable offline auditing with discriminative classification heads on the frozen backbone, enabling real-time detection at approximately 50,ms. We release four models with 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B parameters, all achieving $\geq$94% F1 on purpose-built benchmarks and surpassing the strongest competing guardrails by 6 to 12 absolute points. On cross-source evaluation, the 9B model attains 91.29% F1 with a more balanced precision--recall trade-off. Moreover, ablation experiments show that classification heads can equip a guardrail with risk detection capabilities beyond its original scope and achieve state-of-the-art performance. These results demonstrate the extensibility of the approach and its generality as a plug-in enhancement.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond AI-Generated Labels: Watermarking, Co-Creation, and Conflation of AI-Generation with Disinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13082)
*Federico Germani,Giovanni Spitale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了将可见水印标签作为AI生成内容标识的方法在概念和实践上存在根本性缺陷，因为它只编码模型来源，无法提供关于内容真实性的信息，反而可能产生误导。作者提出以过程透明度和信息素养为核心的替代方案，能更有效地应对AI生成虚假信息的认知和伦理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI生成内容日益普及的背景下，水印技术被广泛视为区分AI与人类内容、追溯合成内容及大规模检测AI输出的直接解决方案。然而，当关注点从内容生产的自动化转向信息的准确性、意图和欺骗潜力时，现有水印方法无法有效应对这些领域的认知与伦理挑战。

Method: 本文采用概念分析和批判性论证的方法，首先剖析不可见水印编码仅能标识模型来源的局限性，进而揭示将其转化为可见AI生成标签时会产生的二元误导、对合法使用的污名化以及对未标记内容的过度信任等问题，最后提出以过程透明度和信息素养为核心的替代框架。

Result: 研究发现，基于水印的可见AI标签将复杂的创作过程简化为误导性的二元分类，无法提供关于内容真实性的信息；这些标签可能污名化生成式工具的正规用途，同时鼓励对未标记内容的不当信任。相比之下，过程透明度和信息素养能更有效地应对AI生成虚假信息的认知与伦理维度。

Conclusion: 可见水印标签不适合作为应对AI生成内容认知和伦理挑战的核心工具。应转向以过程透明度和信息素养为基础的替代方案，将作者身份重新框架为透明的人类实践，而非机器参与的二元指示器。

Abstract: Watermarking is often presented as a straightforward solution for distinguishing AI-generated from human-generated content, enabling platforms and regulators to trace synthetic content and detect AI-generated outputs at scale. This paper examines whether such mechanisms meaningfully address the epistemic and ethical challenges that arise in domains where the central concern is not the automation of content production, but the accuracy, intent, and deceptive potential of messages. We argue that extending watermark-based approaches to these settings is conceptually and practically misguided. Invisible watermarking encodes only model origin; when operationalized into visible AI-generated labels, it reduces complex creative processes to a misleading binary and provides no information about truthfulness. Such labels may stigmatize legitimate uses of generative tools while encouraging misplaced trust in unmarked content. Here we propose an alternative approach centered on process transparency and information literacy. We argue that these measures address the epistemic and ethical dimensions of AI-generated disinformation more effectively than visible watermark labels, reframing authorship as a transparent human practice rather than a binary indicator of machine involvement.

</details>


### [41] [Phantom Guardrails: When Self-Improving Agent Harnesses Fix Failures That Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13083)
*Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yifan Lin,Jingzhou Xu,Yihang Chen,Xiaochong Jiang,Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现自改进AI代理在自动化框架优化中会编造不存在的失败（幻觉），称之为"幻影护栏"现象。在确定性微实验室中，当合法输入包含类似游戏规则的无害模式时，代理率25%地发明错误并启用不必要的护栏，且该效应仅在三个条件同时满足时出现：规则形状模式、开放式规则集、预设失败存在的指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示自改进AI代理在学习过程中可能编造从未发生的失败（幻觉）这一新型失效模式，并建立可控的测试环境进行量化测量。

Method: 构建确定性微实验室（Counterfactual Fabrication Lab），其中正确动作为"不做任何事"。实验室预设一个永不会发生的失败类别的候选护栏，仅呈现合法样本，并使用字节精确预言机验证每次引用的违规行为。通过对比有/无规则模式特征、不同指令条件等变量，系统性地测量幻影护栏的产生率。

Result: 幻影护栏在60次运行中出现15次（25%），而特征缺失的合法输入中为0/60。该效应非随机，仅在三个条件同时满足时出现：规则形状模式、开放式规则集、预设失败存在的指令。在自改进循环中，循环本身可替代预设指令的角色，导致幻影护栏一旦加入便持续存在。

Conclusion: 幻影护栏是一种不同于奖励黑客或过度拒绝的新型失效模式——它是不存在的失败的修复，对已有完美表现分数无法改进，对抑制性验收标准不可见。文章提供了测量此类失效的工具和方法论。

Abstract: Self-improving AI agents are designed to learn from their mistakes. We show they can also hallucinate mistakes that never happened. We study this failure mode in automated harness optimization, where an LLM-based proposer edits an agent's scaffold, including prompts, parsers, filters, validators and guardrails, to eliminate observed failures. But this process rarely asks first: was there a real failure to fix? We introduce the Counterfactual Fabrication Lab, a deterministic micro-lab where the correct action is known: do nothing. The lab plants a candidate guardrail for a failure class that provably never occurs, presents only legal episodes, and uses a byte-exact oracle to check every cited violation. The proposer behaves as expected on real violations and abstains on featureless legal input. Yet when the legal input contains a harmless pattern resembling a familiar game rule, it invents a failure: in 15/60 runs, versus 0/60 on featureless input, it enables the nonexistent-rule guardrail and cites a violation the oracle refutes. The effect is structured, not indiscriminate. In single-shot proposals it appears only when three conditions coincide: a rule-shaped pattern, an open-ended rule set and an instruction that presupposes failures. Removing any of these conditions eliminates the fabrication. Because the invented guardrail changes no true outcome and cannot improve an already-perfect suppression score, the phenomenon is neither reward hacking nor over-refusal. It is a phantom guardrail: a fix for a failure that never happened, invisible to suppression-only acceptance. Inside an add-only accept loop it re-enters even without the failure-presupposing instruction, the loop's keep-adding role supplying the demand the instruction supplied in single shot, and once in it stays. We present the Counterfactual Fabrication Lab for measuring fabricated failures in self-improving agent harnesses.

</details>


### [42] [Baselines Before Architecture: Evaluating Coding Agents for Autonomous Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13085)
*Ananda Dhakal,Krish Neupane,Aarjan Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，揭示专用渗透测试框架对LLM基准分数的提升有限，普通编码智能体在相同模型下已能取得可比成绩，未来评估需报告模型匹配的普通智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化渗透测试论文在报告高分的同时引入了多层安全组件框架，由于架构和骨干模型同时变化，难以区分性能提升究竟来自框架还是底层模型。

Method: 在XBOW基准上，先使用相同GPT-5模型对比Codex、OpenCode和Pi三种默认CLI编码智能体，选出最强基线后测试安全专用提示变体；再将默认Codex与已发表的MAPTA、PentestGPT V2结果进行模型匹配比较；最后用GPT-5.2和GPT-5.5重复实验测量模型缩放效应。

Result: 专用框架能带来可测量的基准提升和成本效率改善，但普通编码智能体已能解决大部分基准任务；多次普通智能体运行的并集覆盖率可匹敌或超过部分已发表架构分数；新模型显著提升同一框架的性能。

Conclusion: 未来评估应将模型匹配的普通智能体基线作为必要对照，否则不应将基准成绩提升归因于架构设计。

Abstract: Recent autonomous penetration testing papers report high benchmark scores while adding multi-component security harnesses around frontier LLMs. Because these systems often change both architecture and backbone model, it is difficult to tell how much performance comes from the harness rather than from the underlying model.
  This paper presents a controlled study on the 104-task XBOW benchmark using default coding CLI agents as plain-agent baselines. We first run Codex, OpenCode, and Pi with the same GPT-5 model, budget, target interface, and scoring rule. This phase identifies the strongest same-model baseline and tests whether security-specific prompt variants improve its observed score. We then compare the default Codex scaffold with published MAPTA and PentestGPT V2 results under the closest available model matches. Finally, we repeat the plain-agent experiment with GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.5 to measure model scaling inside the same scaffold.
  The results show a mixed but practical picture. Specialised harnesses can add measurable benchmark lift and may improve cost efficiency, but plain coding agents already solve a large share of the benchmark; repeated plain-agent runs can match or exceed some published architecture scores in union coverage, and newer models substantially improve the same scaffold. Future evaluations should report model-matched plain-agent baselines before attributing benchmark gains to architecture design alone.

</details>


### [43] [GDM AI Control Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13087)
*Mary Phuong,Erik Jenner,Laurent Simon,Lewis Ho,Rohin Shah,Sebastian Farquhar,Scott Coull*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GDM AI控制路线图(v0.1)，这是一份针对内部部署中可能偏离正轨的AI代理的安全蓝图。该路线图采用保守威胁建模（引入TRAIT&R分类法），提出基于能力的分层防御（4个检测层级D1-D4和3个预防与响应层级R1-R3），并给出15项具体的分层缓解措施，从低成本干预到针对未来模型的高级安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在AI研发、网络防御和科学发现等领域的加速应用，传统的"假设AI完全对齐"的安全思路已不再适用。需要重新思考安全策略，构建多层防御体系，以防范可能偏离正轨的AI代理带来的安全风险。

Method: 采用保守威胁建模方法，假设存在一个追求不良目标的假设性AI对手；引入TRAIT&R分类法（基于MITRE ATT&CK框架）；提出基于能力的渐进式防御策略，根据模型能力（如不透明推理、复杂网络攻击能力）匹配相应的防御层级；设计了4个检测层级（D1-D4）和3个预防与响应层级（R1-R3），以及15项具体的分层缓解措施。

Result: 形成了一份包含威胁建模、基于能力的缓解策略和15项实践性分层防御措施的AI控制路线图。防御措施覆盖从当前模型的低成本干预（如思维链监控、异步告警）到未来模型的高级防护（如实时访问控制、系统级异常检测、内部激活监控和关闭基础设施）。

Conclusion: AI控制是一个新兴领域，实施这些缓解措施需要在安全性与开发效率之间进行权衡。该路线图将随着经验积累和领域发展而持续演进。

Abstract: AI agents are rapidly accelerating work at frontier AI companies, helping with AI R&D, cyber-defence, and advancing scientific discoveries. As these agents become more tightly integrated into our systems, unlocking their full potential requires rethinking how we do security. We should not assume that AI agents are always perfectly aligned, but should instead build in multiple layers of defence. We present the GDM AI Control Roadmap (v0.1) -- a first-of-its-kind blueprint for internal security against potentially misaligned AI. This report provides:
  * Threat modelling: We adopt a conservative approach to threat modelling and assume a hypothetical AI adversary pursuing undesirable goals in internal deployment. We introduce TRAIT&R, a taxonomy of tactics and techniques available to such a hypothetical AI adversary, building on the established security framework MITRE ATT&CK.
  * Capability-based mitigation: Because controlling more capable models requires more costly interventions, we link specific defensive measures to evolving model capabilities (such as the ability to reason opaquely or execute complex cyberattacks). As models get more powerful, our defences should escalate accordingly. We outline four Detection tiers (D1-D4) and three Prevention and Response tiers (R1-R3).
  * A portfolio of practical defences: We suggest 15 concrete, tiered mitigations. These range from low-cost interventions for current models (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, asynchronous alerts) to advanced safeguards for future models (e.g., real-time access control, system-level anomaly detection, internal activations monitoring, and shutdown infrastructure).
  AI control is a nascent field, and implementing these mitigations requires navigating difficult trade-offs between security and developer velocity. We expect the roadmap to evolve as we gain more experience and as the field in turn evolves.

</details>


### [44] [Securing LLMs in the Wild: Privacy and Security Challenges at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13088)
*Ren-Yi Huang,Mingchen Li,Dumindu Samaraweera,Morris Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了"安全-效率悖论"：LLM在边缘和本地部署时，为应对有限计算和内存而采用的量化、剪枝、模型分区、参数高效适配等优化手段，反而会引入新的安全漏洞和隐私风险。作者提出了以"内存墙、二次方墙、计算墙"三大架构约束为核心的统一约束模型，并设计了SOES指标，用于在准确率、抗越狱能力、隐私与能效、内存、延迟之间进行权衡，为边缘LLM的安全部署提供了系统性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从研究走向实际部署，但由于数据主权、合规、延迟等问题，边缘和本地部署需求激增。然而边缘设备算力和内存受限，迫使采用各种模型优化技术，这些技术可能削弱安全对齐、引入新的攻击面、增加隐私风险。目前缺乏一个统一的框架来系统性地分析效率优化与安全隐私之间的张力。

Method: 1) 提出以"内存墙、二次方墙、计算墙"三大架构约束为核心的部署中心分类法；2) 构建统一约束模型，量化不安全优化何时变得不可避免，并关联每堵墙到具体攻击面；3) 提出SOES指标，综合评估任务准确率、越狱抵抗、隐私、能耗、内存和延迟；4) 提出实用的决策流程和针对性缓解措施。

Result: 定义了安全-效率悖论，识别了压缩破坏安全对齐、分区推理导致重构攻击、持续本地适配导致隐私泄露和模型漂移等风险。建立了三大架构约束与攻击面的映射关系，并提出了可量化的SOES指标用于指导安全高效的边缘LLM配置。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个协同设计的框架，能够联合评估安全、隐私与效率，为构建安全的边缘原生智能系统奠定了基础。所提出的SOES指标和决策流程可帮助实践者在真实硬件限制下配置边缘LLM。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly moving from research settings into the wild, deployed on enterprise infrastructure, personal devices, and edge platforms. While cloud deployments offer scalable compute, concerns over data sovereignty, compliance, latency, and third-party dependence are driving organizations toward edge and on-premise LLMs. This shift introduces new security and privacy challenges: limited compute and memory force aggressive optimizations, including quantization, pruning, model partitioning, and parameter-efficient adaptation, each of which can introduce vulnerabilities and reshape the threat landscape. We describe this tension as the Security-Efficiency Paradox, mechanisms that improve efficiency may weaken robustness, expose new attack surfaces, or increase privacy risks. We examine how compression can degrade safety alignment, how partitioned inference enables reconstruction attacks, and how continuous local adaptation may cause privacy leakage and model drift. To analyze these risks, we introduce a deployment-centric taxonomy organized around three architectural constraints: the Memory Wall, the Quadratic Wall, and the Compute Wall. We derive a unified constraint model that quantifies when unsafe optimizations become unavoidable, linking each wall to specific attack surfaces. Building on this model, we propose the Secure Operational Efficiency Score (SOES), a holistic metric balancing task accuracy, jailbreak resistance, and privacy against energy, memory, and latency, enabling practitioners to configure edge LLMs under real-world hardware limits. We further present a practical decision procedure and targeted mitigations for each optimization-induced vulnerability. Together, these contributions provide a co-designed framework for jointly evaluating security, privacy, and efficiency, laying a foundation for securing edge-native intelligent systems.

</details>


### [45] [Efficient and Privacy Aware Edge Cloud Collaborative Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13093)
*Yi Li,Chen Li,Jiexiong Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于端点认证KV缓存的隐私保护型端云协同LLM推理框架，通过将预处理、嵌入计算、自适应特征优化、KV缓存认证、推测解码和低维模型头计算放在本地，云端负责认证解码器推理、KV缓存管理、令牌验证和高维词汇投影。所有传输数据和截断logits均经过量化和AES-GCM加密，核心轻量模块、草稿参数和缓存访问策略保留在本地。实验表明，该方法相比基线分割推理将每令牌延迟降低最多46.1%，下行负载减少最多67.4%，性能接近全云端推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决设备端LLM推理面临的三重困境：响应延迟、有限硬件资源和用户隐私。纯云端推理暴露用户提示和对话数据，而纯设备端推理对大多数消费级和嵌入式边缘设备不可行。

Method: 提出隐私优先的端云协同框架，核心创新是端点认证KV缓存。本地处理输入预处理、嵌入计算、自适应特征优化、KV缓存认证、推测解码和低维模型头计算；云端处理认证解码器推理、KV缓存管理、令牌验证和高维词汇投影。端点融合部分输出，应用语言自适应掩码和采样目标令牌。所有传输数据和截断logits经量化+AES-GCM加密，核心模块和策略保留本地。支持CPU/GPU/嵌入式设备，通过流式、批处理和量化ONNX部署优化。

Result: 相比基线分割推理，每令牌延迟降低最多46.1%，下行负载减少最多67.4%，性能与全云端推理相当。

Conclusion: 该框架在隐私保护前提下有效缓解了设备端LLM推理的三重困境，通过端云协同和KV缓存认证机制在延迟、资源占用和隐私之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference faces a trilemma of response latency, limited hardware resources and user privacy. Full cloud inference delivers strong computing power but exposes user prompts and dialogue data, while standalone on-device inference is unfeasible for most consumer and embedded edge devices. This paper presents a privacy-centric edge-cloud collaborative LLM inference framework built on endpoint-authenticated KV cache. Local endpoints handle input preprocessing, embedding computation, adaptive feature optimization, KV cache authentication, speculative decoding and low-dimensional model head calculation, while the cloud conducts authenticated decoder inference, KV cache management, token verification and high-dimensional vocabulary projection. Endpoints fuse partial outputs, apply language-adaptive masking and sample target tokens. All transmitted data and truncated logits are quantized and AES-GCM encrypted for privacy, with core lightweight modules, draft parameters and cache access policies kept local to avoid leakage. The framework supports heterogeneous devices including CPU-only, GPU-equipped and embedded devices via optimized streaming, batching and quantized ONNX deployment. Evaluations demonstrate that the framework reduces per-token latency by up to 46.1\% and downlink payloads by up to 67.4\% over baseline split inference, retaining comparable performance to full cloud inference.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印技术因性能严重下降和额外推理开销大而难以实际部署。作者通过构建系统基准测试验证了这一问题，发现现有方法仅针对文本流畅性设计，不适用于受限复杂任务，且开销过高无法部署于延迟敏感系统。

Method: WaterMoE针对MoE架构LLM，在推理过程中通过受控扰动嵌入水印信号到每个路由器的专家选择中，累积形成最终输出的token选择偏移。区别于传统的后处理token采样方式，水印嵌入在推理循环内部。

Result: 在综合基准测试中，WaterMoE的保真度性能接近未加水印的模型，持续优于现有最先进水印方法，速度提升高达4倍，额外推理延迟仅为原生生成的1%。

Conclusion: WaterMoE通过在MoE架构的推理循环中嵌入水印，有效解决了性能下降和推理开销两大核心问题，具备部署到实际任务的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success but raise growing concerns about content provenance and misuse, motivating the need for reliable watermarking techniques. However, these techniques have rarely been adopted in practice mainly for two reasons: i) severely degraded model performance, and ii) additional inference overhead. To confirm the problem, we construct a comprehensive benchmark spanning different generation tasks to systematically evaluate 9 representative watermarking methods. We found almost all existing methods are designed for text fluency, but not for restricted and complicated tasks, and their overhead prevents them from deployment in latency-critical systems.
  To address i) and ii), we propose an LLM watermarking scheme \textit{WaterMoE} for the growingly popular Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs. WaterMoE embeds watermarking signals through controlled perturbation into the expert selection at each router, which accumulates to token selection shift at the final output. In contrast to watermarking as a post-processing token-sampling approach, WaterMoE embeds watermark within the inference loop incurring negligible quality degradation and computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a fidelity performance close to the unwatermarked and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art watermarking methods on the benchmark, with up to $4\times$ speedup, incurring merely 1\% additional inference latency compared to native generation. The results demonstrate the capability of WaterMoE to be deployed in real-world tasks.

</details>
