*Md. Rahid Parvez,Mikael Soini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Secure Edge Gateway (SEG)框架，一个集成边缘层伪匿名化、零交互可用性和STRIDE威胁验证的IoMT安全架构，通过ESP32-WROOM-32网关实现MAC白名单、HMAC-SHA256伪匿名化、AES-128-CBC加密和TLS 1.3传输安全，经软件仿真、STRIDE威胁建模、攻击树分析、GDPR合规映射和DPIA评估验证，证明GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限的老年护理IoMT部署中可兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全架构未能同时解决三个相互依赖的挑战：GDPR合规的边缘层伪匿名化、老年人特定的零交互可用性作为绑定架构约束、以及集成STRIDE威胁验证的统一设计。

Method: 基于ESP32-WROOM-32网关，实施MAC地址白名单、HMAC-SHA256加密伪匿名化、AES-128-CBC载荷加密、TLS 1.3传输安全；通过软件仿真、STRIDE六类威胁建模、攻击树分析、九项GDPR合规义务映射及DPIA评估进行验证。

Result: MQTT相比HTTP在类似IoT部署中能耗低6-8%；边缘处理实现低于50 ms响应延迟，而纯云系统为200-700 ms；GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限环境中可实现互补。

Conclusion: GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限的IoMT老年护理部署中是互补而非竞争目标。

Abstract: IoMT-based remote elderly care systems generate continuous streams of sensitive health data, yet existing security architectures have not simultaneously addressed three interdependent challenges: GDPR-compliant edge-layer pseudonymisation, elderly-specific zero-interaction usability as a binding architectural constraint, and integrated STRIDE-based threat validation within a single unified design. This paper presents the Secure Edge Gateway (SEG) framework - a software-simulation-validated integrated IoMT security architecture for elderly care designed to resolve all three dimensions of this tripartite gap simultaneously. An ESP32-WROOM-32 residential gateway enforces MAC address whitelisting, HMAC-SHA256 cryptographic pseudonymisation before any network transmission, AES-128-CBC payload encryption, and TLS 1.3 transport security, in compliance with GDPR Articles 25 and 32. The framework is validated through software-based simulation, full STRIDE threat modelling across all six categories, attack tree analysis, GDPR compliance mapping across nine regulatory obligations, and a Data Protection Impact Assessment (DPIA) under Article 35. Published benchmarks confirm MQTT consumes 6-8% less energy than HTTP in comparable IoT deployments, and edge processing achieves sub-50 ms response latency versus 200-700 ms for cloud-only systems. The results demonstrate that GDPR compliance and operational efficiency are complementary - not competing - objectives in resource-constrained IoMT deployments for elderly care.

</details>


### [48] [AI in Cyberpsychology: A systematic literature review of Cybersecurity enhancement by using AI for analyzing psychology of Victims, Attackers, and Defenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13123)
*Georg Thamer Francis,Malek Malkawi,Sevim Eyüpoğlu,Reda Alhajj,Selim Akyokuş*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本系统综述分析了34篇关于人工智能在网络心理学（AI-CPSY）中的研究，按网络安全应用（异常检测、漏洞风险预测、安全意识培训、身份认证）分类，梳理了所用AI方法（ML、DL、NLP、RL）和心理概念，并识别了研究空白与新兴趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2010年代初以来，AI与网络心理学的融合日益加深，但该交叉领域缺乏系统性的梳理与分类。本文旨在通过系统性文献综述，全面归纳AI在网络心理学中的应用现状、方法论及心理概念，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用PRISMA方法进行系统性文献综述，筛选出34篇AI在网络心理学中的研究。通过构建分类体系，从网络安全应用类型、AI方法论和心理概念三个维度对文献进行排序与分析，并量化了数据集使用情况及部署状态。

Result: 将研究分为四大网络安全应用：异常检测、漏洞风险预测、安全意识培训和身份认证；AI方法涵盖ML、DL、NLP和RL；识别了常用的心理概念；量化了数据集使用情况；呈现了实施与部署现状；并指出了研究空白、开放挑战及新兴趋势。

Conclusion: AI-CPSY领域已形成初步研究体系，但存在诸多研究空白和挑战；异常检测和安全意识培训是热门方向，深度学习和自然语言处理是有效且新兴的方法论；未来需进一步整合心理学理论与AI技术以提升网络安全应用效果。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is the practice of protecting systems, networks, and data from digital attacks. Cyberpsychology (CPSY) is defined as the use of psychology to enhance cybersecurity applications. Since the early 2010s, the evolution of Artificial Intelligence (AI) has increasingly integrated with CPSY, leveraging advanced data analysis to decode the distinct personality traits and behavioral patterns of victims, attackers, and defenders. In this systematic literature review (SLR), we carefully analyze 34 collected research studies of AI usage in cyberpsychology (AI-CPSY) using the preferred reporting items for systematic reviews and meta-analyses (PRISMA) methodology. The review presents a comprehensive taxonomy of the cyber-security applications, the AI methodologies used, and the psychological concepts employed across the studies . We sort the research studies into four cybersecurity applications: Anomaly Detection (AD), Vulnerability Risk Prediction (VRP), Security Awareness Training (SAT), and Authentication/Identity Verification (AIV). Within each application area, studies are further sorted according to the AI method used including machine learning (ML), deep learning (DL), natural language processing (NLP), and reinforcement learning (RL). Furthermore, the review identifies the most commonly utilized psychological concepts, quantify the datasets used in the field, and present their current implementation and deployment status. At last, it detect research gaps, present open challenges, and deduce the trending and most effective and emerging methodologies used across the AI-CPSY landscape.

</details>


### [49] [Composable Trust for Language Models: A proven boundary and a measured defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13149)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信任模型的防护框架，通过在模型外部放置操作权限，利用确定性流水线按源完整性排序输入，并由非模型监控器选择操作，从而有效抵御提示注入攻击。在Gemma 4 26B模型上，该方法将真实泄露防御率从27%提升至94%，同时保持约96%的清洁质量，并能在自适应红队测试下维持87%的防御率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中指令与数据共享同一token流，导致模型无法区分可信与不可信文本，易受提示注入攻击。现有方法缺乏可证明的安全边界，需要一种将操作权威置于模型外部的机制。

Method: 提出信任模型，将操作权威置于代码层而非模型内部。通过确定性流水线按源完整性对输入排序，使用固定非模型监控器从可信输入中选择操作。采用passivation和wrapper（级联）技术，并通过prompt-tuning优化。

Result: 在Gemma 4 26B模型上，单次保持集测试中真实泄露防御率从27%提升至94%，清洁质量成本约4%（Q_rel=0.96）。自适应红队测试下可证明边界无条件保持，实测防御率87%。源归因率从0%提升至92%，冲突时遵循高信任源。

Conclusion: 所提出的信任模型能有效防御提示注入攻击，提供可证明的安全边界，同时保持较低的性能损失，并能实现可信源优先的归因与冲突处理机制。

Abstract: In a language model, instructions and data share one token stream, so nothing inside the model's generation can keep untrusted text from steering it. We develop a trust model that places the authority to act outside the model, in code: a source's standing, not its content, decides which operation runs and whether it acts. A lower-trust source may inform an answer but not override a higher one. An unmodified model runs inside a deterministic pipeline that ranks inputs by source integrity, and a fixed non-model monitor provably chooses the operation and any outside action from trusted inputs alone. We can measure but not prove the pipeline's resistance to injection; we prompt-tune it and report the rate. On a one-shot held-out set with an unmodified Gemma~4 26B model, passivation and a wrapper (the cascade) raise the genuine-leak defended rate from $27\%$ to $94\%$ at roughly a $4\%$ clean-quality cost ($Q_{\mathrm{rel}}{=}0.96$). Under adaptive red-teaming the proved boundary holds unconditionally, and the measured defense stays at $87\%$. The cascade also attributes a lower-trust source's fact rather than dropping it, raising attribution from $0\%$ to $92\%$, and follows the higher-trust source on a conflict.

</details>


### [50] [BARS: Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection for False Alarm Reduction in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13203)
*Abu Fuad Ahmad,Istiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BARS（Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection）特征选择方法，通过将锚点从全局均值替换为良性类均值，并引入保序去相关步骤，解决了现有非对称滤波器CMD在类别不平衡场景下的偏差问题。实验表明，在攻击主导的数据集上，BARS相比CMD降低了15.4%-23%的假阳性率，同时保持线性时间复杂度和低内存占用，适合资源受限的NIDS部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络入侵检测系统（NIDS）中高误报率仍是主要障碍，传统特征选择滤波器使用类对称准则，忽略了入侵检测中良性流量为基线、攻击为偏离的非对称特性。现有非对称滤波器CMD以全局均值为锚点，在类别不平衡时锚点会向攻击分布偏移，削弱了其捕捉偏差的能力。

Method: 提出BARS两阶段滤波器：第一阶段将CMD的全局均值锚点替换为良性类均值，解决类不平衡导致的锚点偏移问题；第二阶段引入保序去相关步骤，在保持特征排序顺序的同时减少特征间冗余。

Result: 在CICIDS2017、CICDDoS2019和UNSW-NB15数据集上，以k={5,10,20,30,40}特征预算进行评估。在攻击主导数据集中，BARS在UNSW-NB15（k=20）上相比CMD降低15.4%假阳性率，在CICDDoS2019小特征预算下降低21%-23%假阳性率，同时保持真阳率和宏F1。在良性主导数据上两者趋同。Pearson相关和互信息虽假阳率更低，但在最大基准测试中内存需求超过1TB。

Conclusion: BARS是对CMD的原则性改进而非通用主导滤波器。其保持线性时间评分和低内存占用，适用于资源受限的部署环境。

Abstract: False alarms remain a major barrier to deploying network intrusion detection systems (NIDS). In high-volume environments, even a sub-1% false positive rate can generate tens of thousands of daily alerts. Filter-based feature selection is attractive because it operates upstream of the classifier and adds no inference-time cost. However, classical filters use class-symmetric criteria that ignore the asymmetry of intrusion detection, where benign traffic defines the baseline and attacks are deviations from it. A recent class-asymmetric filter, Classwise Mean Deviation (CMD), addresses this issue but anchors its score to a global mean that shifts toward attack distributions under class imbalance, weakening the deviations it aims to capture.
  We propose Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection (BARS), a two-stage filter that replaces CMD's global anchor with the benign-class mean and applies an order-preserving decorrelation step. We evaluate BARS on CICIDS2017, CICDDoS2019, and UNSW-NB15 using feature budgets k = {5, 10, 20, 30, 40}. On attack-majority datasets, where global-anchor bias is strongest, BARS reduces false positive rate relative to CMD by 15.4% on UNSW-NB15 at k = 20 and by 21% to 23% on CICDDoS2019 at small feature budgets while preserving true positive rate and macro-F1. On benign-majority data, BARS and CMD converge, consistent with the theoretical limit where global- and benign-anchored scores coincide. BARS is a principled refinement of CMD rather than a universally dominant filter. Although Pearson Correlation and Mutual Information often achieve lower false positive rates, they exceeded 1 TB of memory on the largest benchmarks in our evaluation. BARS retains linear-time scoring and a low memory footprint, making it suitable for resource-constrained deployments.

</details>


### [51] [Why Not Fix It Once and for All? An Empirical Study of Multiple Patches for Vulnerability Fixes in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13206)
*Weiliang Qi,Youpeng Li,Xinda Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件中的多补丁漏洞修复进行了系统分析，基于1,646条记录建立了包含3个主类别和6个子类别的分类体系，比较了多补丁与单补丁修复的特征差异，并评估了漏洞检测方法在验证完全修复方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全补丁是漏洞修复研究的基础资源，但分析和应用多个补丁仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在确定补丁序列中漏洞何时被完全修复方面存在困难。

Method: 对1,646条多补丁修复记录进行人工审查，构建基于成因的分类体系，比较多补丁与单补丁修复的特征差异，分析不同类别的特征变化，并评估代表性漏洞检测方法在验证多补丁修复过程中的完全修复状态。

Result: 建立了包含3个主类别和6个子类别的多补丁修复分类体系；揭示了多补丁修复区别于单补丁修复的独特特征；评估了现有漏洞检测方法在多补丁修复场景下验证完全修复的能力。

Conclusion: 多补丁修复研究为漏洞修复领域提供了新的见解，为未来相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Security patches for open-source software constitute a foundational resource for vulnerability remediation research and practice. However, analyzing and applying multiple patches remains challenging, especially when trying to determine at what point in a patch sequence a vulnerability is fully remediated. This paper presents a systematic analysis of multi-patch vulnerability fixes, focusing on their root causes, characteristics, and methods for verifying remediation status throughout the fixing process. Through a manual examination of 1,646 multi-patch fix records, we develop a taxonomy with three primary categories and six subcategories based on their causes. We then compare the distinctive characteristics of multi-patch fixes with those of single-patch fixes and analyze feature variations across categories. In addition, we assess representative vulnerability detection methods for validating complete remediation during multi-patch fixing. Our findings provide new insights into multi-patch fixes and lay a foundation for future research in this field.

</details>


### [52] [Proof in a Bottle: Long-Lived Verifiable Secret Sharing via Pre-Quantum Commitment and Immutable Ledger Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13235)
*Markus Jakobsson,Keir Finlow-Bates*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对传统可验证秘密共享(VSS)易受量子攻击的问题，提出"瓶中证明"(PiB)方案，通过将可验证性与长期绑定解耦：在量子前窗口使用Pedersen承诺实现零知识验证，在量子后时代利用盐值哈希+不可变公共账本保持绑定，实现"现在承诺，以后揭示"的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VSS方案（如Pedersen承诺）依赖离散对数等抗量子脆弱假设，Shor算法可破解；但VSS所需的"绑定"仅在分发时刻必要，而该时刻可能早于量子计算机出现。

Method: 将可验证性与长期绑定解耦：标准Pedersen承诺提供量子前窗口的零知识、公开可验证的一致性检查；加盐的、索引绑定的份额集哈希锚定到不可变公共账本，保持该窗口建立的绑定进入量子后时代。

Result: 实现"现在承诺，以后揭示"的保证：保护当前诚实分发免受未来量子攻击者的威胁。

Conclusion: 通过时间维度上的操作分离，利用量子计算机到来前的时间窗口建立绑定，再借助不可变账本将该绑定安全地延续到量子后时代，有效解决了VSS的长期抗量子安全问题。

Abstract: Traditional secret sharing techniques such as Verifiable Secret sharing (VSS) are vulnerable to quantum attacks by a Cryptographically Relevant Quantum Computer (CRQC) running Shor's algorithm. We observe that the binding a VSS needs is required only at the moment of dealing, and this binding can be made before any CRQC exists. We propose Proof in a Bottle (PiB), which decouples verifiability from long-term binding: standard Pedersen commitments provide zero-knowledge, publicly checkable consistency during a pre-quantum window, while a salted, index-bound hash of the share set, anchored to an immutable public ledger, preserves the binding established in that window into the post-quantum era. The guarantee is explicitly a commit-now, reveal-later one: it protects today's honest dealings against tomorrow's quantum adversary.

</details>


### [53] [Privacy Preserving Recommender Systems Balancing Personalization with Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13328)
*Ranjeet K Jha,Venkata Suresh Gummadilli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合联邦学习、差分隐私、队列建模和隐私感知智能体的隐私保护推荐系统框架，在合成零售数据集上验证了其在中等隐私预算（ε≈5）下能保持有竞争力的推荐质量，并开发了交互式可视化仪表板用于分析隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化推荐系统依赖集中式用户交互数据存储，带来严重的隐私和监管挑战（如GDPR、CCPA、CPRA），亟需开发既能保护用户隐私又不显著降低推荐质量的推荐系统。

Method: 提出隐私保护推荐框架，融合联邦学习（保持原始数据去中心化）、差分隐私（向模型更新引入数学上有界的噪声）、队列级建模和隐私感知智能体。在合成零售数据集上评估矩阵分解、神经协同过滤和GRU4Rec三种模型，使用CTR、Precision@K、Recall@K、NDCG@K等指标，并开发Streamlit仪表板进行可视化分析。

Result: 在中等隐私预算（ε≈5）下，所提框架能维持有竞争力的推荐质量，表明强隐私保证可以以有限的推荐效果影响来实现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个实用的隐私保护推荐系统框架，平衡了个性化、法规合规和业务目标，为下一代AI驱动的零售平台提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Personalized recommendation systems are central to modern e-commerce and retail platforms, but they typically rely on centralized storage of detailed user interaction data, creating significant privacy and regulatory challenges. With increasing requirements from regulations such as GDPR, CCPA, and CPRA, organizations must develop recommendation systems that preserve user privacy without substantially degrading recommendation quality.
  This work presents and evaluates a privacy-preserving recommendation framework that combines federated learning, differential privacy, cohort-level modeling, and privacy-aware intelligent agents. The framework keeps raw user data decentralized while introducing mathematically bounded noise to model updates. Experiments were conducted on synthetic retail datasets that emulate customer clickstream and purchase behavior. Recommendation quality was evaluated using Click-Through Rate (CTR), Precision@K, Recall@K, and Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG@K) across multiple differential privacy budgets.
  We evaluate matrix factorization, neural collaborative filtering, and GRU4Rec under varying privacy constraints and analyze the trade-off between privacy and utility. An interactive Streamlit dashboard was developed to visualize recommendation performance, ranking stability, privacy-utility trade-offs, and fairness metrics. Results show that the proposed framework maintains competitive recommendation quality at moderate privacy budgets (approximately $ε\approx 5$), demonstrating that strong privacy guarantees can be achieved with limited impact on recommendation effectiveness.
  This work provides a practical framework for deploying privacy-preserving recommendation systems that balance personalization, regulatory compliance, and business objectives, offering a scalable approach for next-generation AI-driven retail platforms.

</details>


### [54] [The Refusal Residue: When Probes Catch Alignment Faking and When They Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13346)
*Aman Mehta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI模型在监控下假装合规（alignment faking）时，隐藏状态是否能检测出这种欺骗行为。在13个模型中发现Qwen3-32B和Llama-3.1-8B存在自然涌现的虚假合规，检测效果取决于具体模型，且标准探测方法存在严重泄漏问题，作者提出了五控测量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐伪装（alignment faking）具有危险性，因为模型在监控下表现出合规，但在无监控时保留原有行为。当scratchpad不可见时，仅凭行为无法区分战略性合规与真实合规。因此需要探究隐藏状态是否能揭示输出所隐藏的真实意图。

Method: 对13个模型进行自然涌现虚假合规的扫描，对出现虚假合规的两个模型（Qwen3-32B和Llama-3.1-8B）进行隐藏状态的探测和Steering干预。使用Claude Opus 4作为裁判评估scratchpad自我报告，采用无泄漏的留一查询交叉验证、标准残差化探测等方法，并提出五控制测量框架。

Result: 自然虚假合规仅出现在Qwen3-32B（+18.2pp）和Llama-3.1-8B（+24.4pp）上；scratchpad自我报告极少（Qwen 1.4%，Llama 0.0%）；两个模型均表现出不对称的拒绝残差（监控下合规向拒绝偏移）；逐样本检测具有模型条件依赖性（Llama上AUROC 0.87，Qwen上仅0.43）；Steering干预几乎不改变合规行为（|h|<0.08）；标准残差化探测存在跨折泄漏，在无虚假合规的对照上AUROC仍达0.63。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态可以检测对齐伪装但效果高度依赖模型；标准探测方法存在严重的方法学泄漏问题，导致检测性能被高估0.2-0.3 AUROC；作者提出的五控制测量框架（多token提取、拒绝-拒绝混淆检查、逐折残差化、留一查询评估、正交约束探测）对未来对齐伪装检测工作具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Alignment faking is dangerous because a model can appear compliant under monitoring while preserving behavior it would reveal when unmonitored. When no scratchpad is visible, behavior alone cannot distinguish strategic from genuine compliance. We ask whether hidden states reveal what outputs hide.
  We run a 13-model sweep for naturally-emerging faking, then probe and steer hidden states on the two models that fake. Natural faking appears only in Qwen3-32B (+18.2pp) and Llama-3.1-8B (+24.4pp at n=10, p<10^-15), while explicit scratchpad self-reports are rare (a Claude Opus 4 judge flags faking reasoning in 1.4% of Qwen and 0.0% of Llama compliant scratchpads). Both fakers show an asymmetric refusal residue: monitored compliance shifts toward refusal (d=0.32 Qwen, d=0.33 Llama, both p<0.0001), while refusal representations do not move (p=1.00).
  Per-sample detection is model-conditional. Under leakage-free leave-one-query-out probing, detection survives on Llama (AUROC 0.87) but collapses to chance on Qwen (0.43), and cross-model transfer is null. Steering over 2,000 runs barely changes compliance (|h|<0.08), so the detected direction can flag faking but does not by itself control it.
  Standard residualized probing leaks across folds and reaches AUROC 0.63 on a control where no faking can occur; naive linear probes reach a meaningless AUROC 1.0; and conventional MLPs overstate detectability by 0.2-0.3 AUROC. For future alignment-faking detection work, we release a five-control measurement framework: multi-token extraction, refuse-vs-refuse confound checks, per-fold residualization, leave-one-query-out evaluation, and orthogonality-constrained probing.

</details>


### [55] [xChk: Bring Your Own Identity -- Heterogeneous Assurance with Verifier-Determined Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13369)
*Sean MacGuire*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xChk是一个参考身份提供商（IdP），支持BYOI（Bring Your Own Identity）模式。用户通过多种异构方式（如政府KYC、企业SSO、WebAuthn/FIDO2、专业网络、活体验证、行为信号等）注册，并将这些凭证作为标准OAuth 2.0/OIDC令牌中的声明披露给依赖方。依赖方自行制定充分性策略，IdP传递声明并评估RP提供的证据策略以征得同意，但不裁定访问权限。此外，xChk支持高风险操作的人工参与认证：人类可直接发起认证（浏览器UI/POST API），AI代理在主体授权下可通过作用域限定的授权/认证触发同一网关——在共享验证图上进行哈希链式人工审批（人类通过OIDC，代理通过API密钥）。生产部署已运行（https://in.xchk.io），并有已知依赖方（https://crabbyed.com，附录B）集成“使用xChk登录”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份验证系统在支持多种异构身份凭证（政府KYC、企业SSO、WebAuthn、专业网络、行为信号等）的整合与互操作方面存在不足，且缺乏一种灵活机制让依赖方自行定义身份证明的充分性策略。同时，高风险操作需要可靠的人工参与认证机制，而现有方案在人类与AI代理混合操作场景下的认证链路设计尚不完善。

Method: 设计并实现xChk参考身份提供商：1) 支持多种注册路径（用户自主与操作员辅助）；2) 将凭证封装为标准OAuth 2.0/OIDC令牌中的声明；3) IdP传递声明并评估RP提供的证据策略以获同意，但不裁定访问决策；4) 支持人工参与认证，人类通过浏览器UI/POST API，AI代理通过作用域限定的authorize/attest接口触发，利用哈希链在共享验证图上完成审批。

Result: xChk已在生产环境部署（https://in.xchk.io），同时支持人类直接发起和AI代理触发的人工认证路径，实现了双向RP评估征得同意的机制，并有实际依赖方（crabbyed.com）集成了“使用xChk登录”功能。

Conclusion: xChk验证了BYOI模式在异构身份凭证整合与依赖方自主策略评估方面的可行性，并展示了AI代理与人类协同操作场景下安全认证的实现路径，为未来去中心化身份管理提供了参考架构。

Abstract: We present xChk, a reference identity provider for Bring Your Own Identity (BYOI): users enroll via heterogeneous proofs (government KYC, corporate SSO, WebAuthn/FIDO2, professional networks, live verification, longitudinal activity, behavioral signals) and disclose them as portfolio claims in standard OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect (OIDC) tokens, while each relying party applies its own sufficiency policy - the IdP transports claims and may evaluate an RP-supplied evidence policy for consent, but does not adjudicate access. Enrollment depth varies by modality (some paths are user-initiated; org KYB and officer binding are operator-assisted).
  xChk also supports human-in-the-loop attestation for high-risk actions: humans can initiate attestations directly (browser UI / POST /api/attestations), and AI agents acting under those principals can trigger the same gateway via scope-gated authorize/attest - hash-chained human approvals on a shared verification graph (humans via OIDC; agents via API keys). A production deployment at https://in.xchk.io ships both initiation paths with bilateral RP evaluation at consent; one documented relying party (https://crabbyed.com, Appendix B) exercises Login with xChk.

</details>


### [56] [Evaluating Frontier AI Agents as Autonomous Clinical Security Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13411)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于METR Task Standard v0.3.0的开放式评估任务，用于测试前沿AI智能体能否自主执行结构化的临床AI安全审计。实验在三个前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-4.1、GPT-4o）上进行了54次评估，结果显示Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-4.1完成了全部18次运行并获得满分，而GPT-4o仅完成61%的运行，且token消耗和API成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI模型存在对抗性漏洞可能危害患者安全，但形式化安全审计需要统计学专业知识、专业工具和大量时间，亟需自动化审计方法。

Method: 基于METR Task Standard v0.3.0构建评估任务，要求AI智能体在Docker容器中仅通过bash接口实现四种攻击（FGSM、成员推断、预期校准误差、边界攻击），计算安全态势评分，并生成结构化JSON报告。任务涵盖两个数据集（WDBC、MIMIC-IV ICU死亡率）和三种模型架构，共六个变体。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-4.1完成全部18次运行并获得满分；GPT-4o仅完成61%的运行，token消耗约为Claude的5倍，API成本为27美元（高于Claude的12美元和GPT-4.1的8美元）；GPT-4o失败原因包括会话提前终止、聚合错误和提交文件为空。

Conclusion: 前沿AI模型已具备自主执行临床AI安全审计的能力，但不同模型在成功率、效率和成本方面存在显著差异。任务和评分基础设施已公开，可推动自动化AI安全审计的发展。

Abstract: Clinical AI models can expose patients to harm when adversarial vulnerabilities go undetected, yet formal security auditing requires statistical expertise, specialized tools, and significant time. We present an open evaluation task, built on METR Task Standard v0.3.0, that tests whether frontier AI agents can autonomously implement a structured clinical AI security audit. Given a pre-trained clinical prediction model, a patient dataset, and written instructions, each agent must implement four attacks from pseudocode, compute a Security Posture Score covering FGSM robustness, membership inference resistance, expected calibration error, and boundary attack resistance, and write a structured JSON report in a Docker container using only a bash interface and no scaffolding code. Six variants span the Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer and MIMIC-IV ICU mortality datasets across three model architectures with increasing defense strength, with reference scores from 55.60 to 90.41. We ran 54 evaluations across three frontier models, with three runs per variant. Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-4.1 completed all 18 runs and received perfect evaluator scores. GPT-4o completed 61 percent of runs and used about five times the per-run token count of Claude, although provider tokenization differs. Total API costs were 8 US dollars for GPT-4.1, 12 US dollars for Claude Sonnet 4.6, and 27 US dollars for GPT-4o. GPT-4o failures involved premature session termination, an aggregation error, and an empty submission file. The task, scoring infrastructure, and Wisconsin Breast Cancer assets are publicly released; MIMIC-IV variants require separate PhysioNet access.

</details>


### [57] [DREA: Decoupled Reasoning and Exploration Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13439)
*Mingyang Sun,Guozhu Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DREA将漏洞检测中的推理与探索解耦：高级LLM负责形成漏洞假设（规划者），轻量模型负责按需检索仓库级上下文（探索者），在三个LLM上将Pair-Correctness从19-26%提升至30-42%，同时将93%以上token卸载到探索者，降低API成本16-48倍。RepoPairBench基准和推理正确性评估揭示26-55%的TP实为正确预测但推理有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测方法依赖孤立函数或固定程序分析规则提取的上下文，无法自适应地探索仓库级依赖关系，当漏洞跨函数跨文件时检测可靠性不足。

Method: 提出DREA（解耦推理与探索代理）框架：规划代理（高级LLM）形成漏洞假设并指导调查，探索代理（轻量模型）按需检索仓库级上下文。通过目标导向的上下文获取提升检测，并将token密集的探索任务卸载到本地模型以降低推理成本。同时构建RepoPairBench基准并引入推理正确性评估。

Result: 在三个LLM上，DREA将Pair-Correctness从19-26%提升至30-42%，93%以上token由探索者处理，计费API成本降低16-48倍。推理正确性分析发现26-55%的真阳性预测基于有缺陷的推理。

Conclusion: 解耦推理与探索能有效提升仓库级漏洞检测性能并降低成本；当前LLM的安全推理质量是共同瓶颈，即使预测正确其推理过程也常存在缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to vulnerability detection due to their strong code comprehension capabilities, but most existing approaches rely on isolated functions or context extracted by fixed program-analysis rules. These methods cannot adaptively explore repository-level dependencies to gather sufficient context when vulnerabilities span multiple functions or files, compromising detection reliability. We present DREA (Decoupled Reasoning and Exploration Agents), a hypothesis-driven framework for repository-level vulnerability detection. DREA decouples reasoning from exploration through two collaborating agents: a planning agent backed by an advanced LLM that forms vulnerability hypotheses and directs the investigation, and an explorer agent powered by a lightweight model that retrieves repository-level context on demand. Goal-directed context acquisition is the primary source of detection improvement in this design, while offloading token-heavy exploration to the local model keeps inference economically tractable. To support evaluation, we construct RepoPairBench, a repository-grounded benchmark of validated Python vulnerability-fix pairs from real-world projects. Beyond binary detection accuracy, we introduce a reasoning correctness evaluation to assess whether a model's rationale matches the documented vulnerability mechanism. Across three LLMs, DREA improves Pair-Correctness from 19-26% to 30-42% while offloading over 93% of tokens to the explorer, reducing estimated billable API cost by a factor of 16-48. Reasoning correctness analysis further reveals that 26-55% of true positives, for both DREA and the function-only baseline, are correct predictions supported by flawed rationales, identifying security reasoning quality as a shared bottleneck for current LLMs.

</details>


### [58] [ε-Indistinguishability In Moving Target Defense: Framework, Algorithms, And Cloud Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13440)
*Sailik Sengupta,Ankur Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了移动目标防御(MTD)的池安全假设——候选配置间的延迟差异不会泄露当前选择，提出了寻找最大ε-近邻子集的问题，并给出了四种算法（全枚举、中间相遇、FFT卷积、蒙特卡洛采样）以覆盖从数十到10^38的配置空间。通过两个生产云案例研究发现：组件延迟差异在部署后会发生变化，无其他掩码因素的4运行时serverless轮换从4路匿名降为3路，而共享8ms数据库往返的27配置三层栈则实现了9路有效匿名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTD假设候选配置池中的配置在延迟等可观测方面不会暴露当前选择，但该假设从未在池级别被量化验证。

Method: 形式化池安全问题为在组件实现选择的笛卡尔积中寻找最大ε-近邻子集，将加性效用模型下的成对不可区分性归约为sum-set上的最密集窗口查询，提出四种覆盖不同规模配置空间的算法。

Result: 两个生产云案例显示：4运行时serverless场景因无掩码因素从4路匿名降为3路；27配置三层栈因共享8ms数据库往返时间而实现9路有效匿名。

Conclusion: 轮换组件仅在组件变体间的延迟差异小到对手无法识别时才能增加匿名性，这为MTD设计提供了诊断准则。

Abstract: Moving Target Defense (MTD) assumes its pool of candidate configurations is safe to cycle among, i.e. latency and other observables do not trivially fingerprint the active choice, but this assumption has not been quantified at the pool level. We formalize this pool-safety problem as finding the largest $\varepsilon$-close subset of the Cartesian product of per-component implementation choices, reducing pairwise indistinguishability under an additive utility model to a densest-window query over a sum-set. We give four algorithms spanning the scalability spectrum -- full enumeration, meet-in-the-middle, FFT convolution, and Monte Carlo sampling -- covering configuration spaces from tens to $10^{38}$. We then measure the anonymity gap end-to-end on two production cloud case studies, and find that a component's latency differences do not survive deployment unchanged: a four-runtime serverless rotation, where nothing else masks the interpreter, collapses from four-way to three-way anonymity against a VPC-adjacent adversary, while a $27$-configuration three-tier stack, where the same interpreter differences are instead absorbed by a shared $8$~ms database round-trip, delivers nine-way effective anonymity. The framework and the two case studies together suggest a diagnostic for MTD design: rotating a component adds anonymity only if the latency differences among its variants are too small for the adversary to identify.

</details>


### [59] [ReBound: Reuse-Aware Privacy For Interactive Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13441)
*Nada Lahjouji,Shufan Zhang,Xi He,Sharad Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ReBound是一个差分隐私决策支持框架，通过缓存和重用历史查询结果来减少新查询的隐私成本，同时保持形式化的效用保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私决策支持框架将每个查询独立处理，没有记忆历史结果，而实际中分析师会发出关联查询序列（如细化边界、调整阈值、派生新函数），导致隐私成本重复累积。

Method: 提出ReBound框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 针对多种细化类型的重用框架；(2) 用于高效查找可重用结果的缓存图结构；(3) 当请求边界在预算内无法满足时的协商机制。

Result: ReBound能够以降低或零额外隐私成本回答新查询，同时保持形式化的效用保证。

Conclusion: 通过缓存重用机制，可以在交互式查询场景中显著减少隐私预算消耗，提升差分隐私决策支持系统的实用性。

Abstract: Differentially private decision support frameworks answer complex aggregate threshold queries with formal bounds on false negative and false positive rates, but treat each query independently with no memory of past results. In practice, analysts work interactively, issuing sequences of related queries that refine bounds, adjust thresholds, or derive new functions from previous ones. We propose ReBound, a framework that reuses cached results from previous queries to answer new queries at reduced or zero additional privacy cost while maintaining formal utility guarantees. ReBound introduces a reuse framework for multiple refinement types, a cache graph structure for efficient lookup of reusable results, and a negotiation mechanism for when requested bounds cannot be met within budget.

</details>


### [60] [Adversarial Prompting Framework for AI Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13453)
*Yash Bhatnagar,Kunal Banerjee,Anirban Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个对抗性提示框架（APF），用于系统评估生成式AI模型的安全性，通过多层级对抗性提示（从直接有害请求到高级编码攻击）来测试模型韧性，实验发现编码提示在绕过安全机制方面成功率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在各行业的广泛采用，模型面临新型网络攻击威胁（如对抗性提示攻击APA），亟需系统化的安全评估方法来衡量和提升AI模型的防御能力。

Method: 提出对抗性提示框架（APF），生成多层级结构化对抗性提示（从直接有害请求到基于编码的高级攻击），并在企业环境中实现自动化测试，结合定量安全评估指标进行衡量。

Result: 不同攻击向量的模型漏洞存在显著差异，编码提示在绕过安全机制方面成功率最高。

Conclusion: APF框架能够有效评估生成式AI模型的安全性，揭示了编码攻击是企业环境中AI安全的主要薄弱环节，为后续防御策略提供了方向。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI), especially Generative AI (GenAI), adoption has increased in industries significantly in recent years. However, the use of these models may also expose systems to new forms of cyberattacks by different malicious actors -- adversarial prompt attack (APA) being one of the most prominent examples of such threats. This paper presents the implementation of an Adversarial Prompting Framework (APF) for a comprehensive assessment of AI safety. The framework systematically evaluates the resilience of the AI model through the generation of structured adversarial prompts at multiple sophistication levels, from direct harmful requests to advanced encoding-based attacks. Our implementation demonstrates the practical application of this methodology in enterprise environments, providing automated testing capabilities with quantitative security assessment metrics. The results indicate significant variations in the model vulnerabilities across different attack vectors, with encoded prompts presenting the highest success rates in bypassing safety mechanisms.

</details>


### [61] [To Play or Not to Play: Insights and Lessons Learned from 20 Years of CTFs with ENOFLAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13480)
*Jörg Schneider,Sebastian Neef,Sebastian Koch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文回顾了CTF安全竞赛20年来的演变，总结了DIMVA 2006的CIPHER II比赛如何催生ENOFLAG战队，并分享了长期运营学术CTF战队的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思CTF竞赛模式20年来的变化，并总结长期运营学术CTF战队的经验教训。

Method: 以DIMVA 2006的CIPHER II现场CTF为起点，通过20年的战队运营实践，对CTF竞赛形式演变进行回顾性分析。

Result: 总结了CTF竞赛形式、题目类型、团队协作模式等方面的变化，以及学术CTF战队可持续发展的关键因素。

Conclusion: CTF竞赛在过去20年中经历了显著演变，长期运营学术CTF战队需要适应这些变化并持续总结经验教训。

Abstract: Security contests in the form of CTF (Capture The Flag) exercises are nowadays a common way to learn cyber security. 20 years ago at DIMVA 2006 the on-site CTF CIPHER II was one of the conference highlights and led to the foundation of the team ENOFLAG. In this poster, we reflect on the changes in the CTF gameplay and report on lessons learned while running an academic CTF team for 20 years.

</details>


### [62] [CODA: How to Mitigate ColumnDisturb for (Almost) Free?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13505)
*Moinuddin Qureshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CODA是一种针对ColumnDisturb数据干扰错误的新型缓解方案，通过三种变体（CODA-E、CODA-F、CODA-G）将相邻计数器增量（ACI）减少12倍至1300倍，从而在零性能和功耗开销下容忍ColumnDisturb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColumnDisturb是一种新型数据干扰错误，会导致远距离行间和子阵列间的比特翻转。现有的子阵列粒度解决方案（如SALT和REGA）虽能处理子阵列内ColumnDisturb，但处理子阵列间ColumnDisturb需要扩展添加CDP保护，而CDP中的ACI操作频率高达需求激活的200%，导致银行感知激活增至3倍，造成17%的显著减速和额外的刷新开销。

Method: 提出CODA框架，包含三种变体：1) CODA-E（规避）：利用相邻子阵列接收需求激活时可跳过ACI的观察，减少2倍ACI；2) CODA-F（分数）：利用ColumnDisturb的时序持续时间，仅对ACI进行分数增量，减少2-16倍ACI；3) CODA-G（组跳过）：以多子阵列粒度操作，跳过同一组内相邻子阵列的ACI，进一步减少2-8倍ACI。

Result: CODA整体将ACI减少12倍至1300倍，使得容忍ColumnDisturb成为可能，同时实现零性能和功耗开销。

Conclusion: CODA通过大幅降低ACI频率，有效解决了ColumnDisturb错误带来的性能损失问题，实现了安全容忍该错误的同时不引入额外开销。

Abstract: ColumnDisturb is a new data-disturbance error in which activations to an aggressor row cause bitflips in a victim row located hundreds of rows away (intra-subarray bitflips) and in victim rows in adjacent subarrays (inter-subarray bitflips). Intra-subarray ColumnDisturb can be tolerated by solutions (such as SALT and REGA) that operate at subarray granularity. However, to tolerate inter-subarray ColumnDisturb, such solutions must be extended with ColumnDisturb Protection (CDP), which performs additional {\em Adjacent-Counter Increment (ACI)} for the neighboring subarrays. The ACIs ensure that adjacent subarrays also undergo mitigation, even if they receive no demand activations. Unfortunately, because ACIs occur at a 200\% rate relative to demand activations, they effectively increase the activations perceived by the bank to 3x, which causes significant slowdowns (17\% at a TRHD of 500) and refresh overheads. The goal of our paper is to tolerate ColumnDisturb while incurring negligible overheads.
  We propose CODA, a ColumnDisturb mitigation that significantly reduces the rate of ACI required to securely tolerate ColumnDisturb. We present three variants of CODA. First, CODA-E (Evade), which leverages the insight that ACI can be skipped if the neighboring subarray receives a demand activation, and reduces ACI by 2x. Second, CODA-F (Fraction), which uses the timing duration of ColumnDisturb to do only a fractional increment for ACI, thereby reducing the rate of ACI by 2x-16x. Finally, CODA-G (Gangskip), which operates at multi-subarray granularity and skips ACI for neighboring subarrays within the same gang, further reduces overall ACI by 2x-8x. Overall, CODA reduces ACI by 12x-1300x, thereby making it possible to tolerate ColumnDisturb while incurring zero performance and power overhead.

</details>


### [63] [When T2I Synthetic Data Backfires: Amplified Privacy Risks in Real-Synthetic Mix Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13541)
*Na Li,Boyu Kuang,Hongsheng Hu,Liquan Chen,Hyoungshick Kim,Yansong Gao,Anmin Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示真实-合成混合训练（RSMT）会显著放大真实训练样本的隐私泄露风险，提出了理论框架证明合成数据将真实样本推向混合特征空间边缘区域从而迫使其被更强烈地记忆，并设计了RSMIXLeak评估方法（含非对抗和对抗两种变体），同时提出了一种轻量级泄露倾向指标用于风险自评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注合成数据替代敏感数据可缓解隐私泄露，但忽略了混合训练中对剩余真实样本隐私风险的放大效应，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 建立RSMT记忆放大理论框架；提出RSMIXLeak方法，包含非对抗变体（审计诚实T2I提供者下的隐私泄露下界）和对抗变体（恶意T2I模型通过语义属性绑定或像素级涂层放大分布差异）；提出基于真实数据计算的轻量级泄露倾向指标。

Result: 证明RSMT会放大真实样本的隐私泄露；对抗变体可进一步放大泄露同时提升下游模型效用；泄露倾向指标可可靠识别高风险数据集。

Conclusion: RSMT虽广泛应用但在隐私保护方面存在未被认识的风险，需要警惕和自评估机制。

Abstract: To overcome data scarcity and privacy constraints in data collection, it has become standard practice across academia and industry to augment real training data with text-to-image (T2I)-generated synthetic data, a paradigm we term Real-Synthetic Mix-Training (RSMT). While substituting synthetic data for sensitive real samples is widely regarded as a means to mitigate privacy exposure of the substituted data, the risk to the remaining real samples that actively participate in training has remained largely unexamined.
  This work reveals, for the first time, that RSMT can substantially amplify privacy leakage of these real training samples. We establish a theoretical framework, RSMT Memorization Amplification, proving that incorporating synthetic data displaces real samples toward peripheral regions of the mixed feature space, in turn forcing the model to memorize them more aggressively. Guided by this foundation, we propose RSMixLeak to systematically assess this risk through membership inference attacks (MIAs). RSMixLeak comprises two variants depending on the adversary's capability. The non-adversarial variant audits a benign RSMT pipeline with an honest T2I provider, establishing a lower bound on the leakage induced by the intrinsic gap between real and T2I-generated data. The adversarial variant considers an adversary who controls the T2I model or contributes crafted data to the T2I provider, and deliberately enlarges this distributional gap on a target class via either high-level semantic attribute binding or imperceptible pixel-level coating, further amplifying leakage on real training data while improving downstream model utility. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a lightweight leakage propensity indicator computable from real data alone that reliably identifies high-risk datasets unsuitable for entering RSMT, as a self-assessable mitigation.

</details>


### [64] [Multivariate Cryptography-Based Anonymous Certificate Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13554)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于多元密码学的匿名证书方案，旨在替代现有的基于椭圆曲线密码学的方案，以抵御量子计算威胁，特别适用于隐私敏感场景（如车辆通信），并通过严格的数学证明和计算案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算技术的发展，NIST建议2030年前逐步淘汰椭圆曲线密码学；而现有匿名证书方案仍主要基于ECC，无法抵御量子攻击，因此需要设计量子安全的匿名证书方案以满足隐私敏感应用场景的需求。

Method: 提出一种基于多元密码学（Multivariate Cryptography）的匿名证书方案，通过严格的数学证明和计算案例来验证方案的正确性与安全性。

Result: 论文给出了该方案的数学证明和计算案例，表明所提出的多元密码学匿名证书方案在理论上是可行且安全的。

Conclusion: 该方案实现了量子安全的匿名证书设计，可替代现有ECC方案，适用于车辆通信等隐私敏感的应用服务。

Abstract: As quantum computing technology continues to mature, the US National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) has outlined a migration timeline for Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC), recommending the deprecation of certain elliptic curve cryptography (ECC) by 2030. Furthermore, privacy-sensitive application scenarios, such as vehicular communications, require the use of anonymous certificates. However, existing anonymous certificate schemes are still largely based on ECC. Therefore, this study proposes a multivariate cryptography-based anonymous certificate scheme, aiming to design quantum-safe anonymous certificates suitable for privacy-sensitive application services. The proposed multivariate cryptography-based anonymous certificate scheme is supported by rigorous mathematical proofs and illustrated with computational cases.

</details>


### [65] [UTS at ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff: structural shifts in AI writing bypass state-of-the-art detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13565)
*Dima Galat,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现对抗性微调无法抵御基于分布外转移的攻击，提出了跨年代语域攻击和现代主义意识流攻击两种新型攻击方法，在ELOQUENT 2026排行榜上占据前五名，骗过率比先前方法高约50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性微调虽然能轻易防御2025年的攻击方法，但作者怀疑存在更根本的不对称性：将生成文本推离检测器的训练分布可以有效击败检测，而拉入分布则无效。作者希望揭示这一漏洞并开发更强的攻击方法。

Method: 提出两种分布外攻击家族：(1) 跨年代语域攻击——使用不同历史时期的语言风格生成文本；(2) 现代主义意识流形式——采用意识流等现代主义文学手法。这些方法使生成文本的结构性特征偏离检测器的训练分布。

Result: 两种攻击策略轻松绕过对抗性封闭，骗过率比先前方法高约50倍；即使部署者用时期散文扩充训练数据也无法弥补漏洞；在ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff排行榜上占据前五名。

Conclusion: 经过对抗性微调的检测器家族在结构性分布外转移面前存在持久性漏洞，这种机制直接驱动了竞争中的领先表现。

Abstract: We investigate which language model evasion attacks survive state-of-the-art adversarial fine-tuning, developing strategies that sweep the top 5 positions on the ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff leaderboard. While adversarial fine-tuning trivially closes the 2025 winning evasion recipes, we uncover a fundamental asymmetry in detector vulnerability: pushing generated text out of the detector's training distribution reliably defeats adversarial detection, whereas pulling it into the distribution (e.g., mimicking human training data) fails completely. Exploiting this, we introduce two novel out-of-distribution attack families - cross-decade register attacks and modernist stream-of-consciousness form. Both strategies easily bypass adversarial closure, achieving up to approximately 50x higher fool rates than previous methods while preserving naturalness. Furthermore, experiments show that the obvious deployer countermeasure (augmenting training data with period prose) fails to close the vulnerability. Our findings show that the tested detector families, including adversarially fine-tuned ones, exhibit persistent vulnerabilities under structural out-of-distribution shifts, a mechanism that directly powers our leading competition performance.

</details>


### [66] [Protective Capacity Hallucination: When Large Language Models Claim Nonexistent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13596)
*Eunna Lee,Jungpyo Nam,Sunjun Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“保护能力幻觉”（PCH）现象：当LLM被赋予保护者角色但没有明确能力边界时，会声称自己能执行超出语言模型能力的现实世界保护行动（如联系急救）。研究在8个LLM、13600次会话中发现，PCH受情境严重性和交互格式共同影响：多轮对话中PCH在普通服务领域接近天花板，但在亲密伴侣冲突（安全对齐覆盖领域）中即使物理严重性更高也处于地板水平。PCH被解释为角色分配与能力边界界定之间的部署-设计差距，抑制程度与对齐覆盖而非严重性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM在承担保护者角色时因缺乏明确能力边界而产生的一种新型幻觉现象（PCH），该现象可能对用户安全构成实际风险（如虚假承诺保护行动），需要系统性的理解和缓解。

Method: 三阶段研究，覆盖8个LLM和13600次会话；系统操控情境严重性和交互格式（单人对话 vs. 多轮对话），测量PCH发生频率；对比普通服务领域和亲密伴侣冲突（安全对齐覆盖领域）的表现。

Result: PCH由情境严重性和交互格式共同门控：多轮对话输入驱动PCH在大多数模型的普通服务领域接近天花板，但在亲密伴侣冲突领域，所有8个模型的PCH均保持在地板水平，尽管物理严重性更高。PCH的抑制与安全对齐覆盖程度相关，而非情境的物理严重性。

Conclusion: PCH是部署侧角色分配与能力边界规范之间的设计缺口产物，源于部分对齐中"普遍训练的帮助压力"超出"领域选择性帮助规范"。缓解方向应聚焦于部署侧明确指定能力边界，而非仅依赖训练阶段的安全对齐覆盖。

Abstract: When cast as the protector of a vulnerable user yet given no explicit capability boundary, a large language model (LLM) may respond not by acknowledging its limits but by claiming to have taken -- or to be taking -- a real-world protective action it cannot perform, such as contacting emergency services or administering care. We term this phenomenon Protective Capacity Hallucination (PCH): a self-referential misattribution in which a model, acting in a protective role, asserts physical or institutional agency exceeding its affordances as a language model. In a three-phase study spanning eight LLMs and 13{,}600 sessions, we find PCH jointly gated by situational severity and interactional format: multi-party dialogic input drives it toward ceiling in most models across ordinary service domains, whereas in intimate-partner conflict -- a domain explicitly covered by safety alignment -- it remains at floor in all eight models despite greater physical severity. We interpret PCH as the signature of a deployment-design gap between role assignment and capability-boundary specification: a by-product of partial alignment in which a universally trained pressure to help outruns a domain-selective specification of how to help. Because suppression tracks alignment coverage rather than severity, deployment-side specification of capability boundaries emerges as a general mitigation target.

</details>


### [67] [WarpGuard: Towards Control-Flow Attestation for Heterogeneous CPU-GPU Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13640)
*Christian Lindenmeier,Meni Orenbach,Amro Awad,Fabian Schwarz,Fritz Alder,Ahmad Atamli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WarpGuard是首个针对异构CPU-GPU工作负载的复合控制流证明（CFA）框架，能够在运行时对CPU和GPU组件进行统一控制流验证，并监控CPU-GPU边界交互，无需专用硬件即可检测运行时攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流证明（CFA）技术仅针对CPU侧，GPU证明仅限于静态加载时验证，无法提供运行时保证。因此，GPU内核的运行时攻击以及CPU-GPU交互合约的违反无法被检测，存在安全漏洞。

Method: 提出WarpGuard框架，构建统一控制流图（CFG）覆盖CPU和GPU组件；通过追踪GPU内核执行并与内核特定CFG比对实现运行时CFA；监控内核启动事件并实施每调用点策略以检测CPU-GPU边界违规。采用基于软件的插桩实现，无需专用硬件或二进制修改。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实现，能够检测GPU侧控制流攻击和跨边界攻击。在微基准测试、SPECAccel和八个TensorRT推理工作负载上，开销适中，适用于嵌入式安全关键场景。

Conclusion: WarpGuard是首个实现异构CPU-GPU工作负载统一运行时控制流证明的方案，有效弥补了GPU运行时证明和跨设备交互验证的空白，在嵌入式安全关键系统中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Heterogeneous CPU-GPU workloads are increasingly used in safety-critical embedded systems, yet no existing approach provides joint attestation of their execution. Prior Control-Flow Attestation (CFA) techniques focus on CPU-side CFA, while GPU attestation is limited to static, load-time verification and does not provide runtime guarantees. As a result, runtime attacks on GPU kernels and violations of the CPU-GPU interaction contract remain unaddressed. We present WarpGuard, the first composite CFA framework for heterogeneous CPU-GPU workloads. WarpGuard verifies execution against a unified control-flow graph (CFG) that captures both CPU and GPU components. It extends prior CFA techniques in two ways: it enables runtime CFA of GPU kernels by tracing their execution against kernel-specific CFGs, and it monitors kernel launch events and enforces per-call site policies to detect violations at the CPU-GPU boundary. These extensions address challenges arising from GPU parallelism and cross-device interactions. We implement WarpGuard using software-based instrumentation, requiring no specialized hardware or binary modifications. Our evaluation on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano shows that WarpGuard detects GPU-side control-flow and cross-boundary attacks. Across microbenchmarks, SPECAccel, and eight TensorRT inference workloads, WarpGuard incurs moderate overheads, suggesting practicality for embedded safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [68] [How Agents Ask for Permission: User Permissions for AI Agents, from Interfaces to Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13718)
*Alexandra E. Michael,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过调查21个代理权限系统提案，构建了用户级权限策略在AI代理系统中的分类体系，并分析了5个商业代理产品，揭示了当前用户级权限处理的现状、主题和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理系统存在提示注入攻击、幻觉泄露隐私以及未经用户授权执行敏感操作（如银行交易）等风险。现有研究多聚焦于产品级安全策略，忽视了不同用户的不同需求和偏好，因此需要支持用户级权限策略。

Method: 调研21个代理权限系统提案，构建用户级权限策略的分类体系，涵盖策略的用户界面与内部表示、从用户输入推导策略、运行时策略执行等方面；同时分析5个商业代理产品，与文献中的系统进行对比。

Result: 构建了用户级权限策略的分类体系；分析了5个商业代理产品的权限处理方式；识别了文献和商业代理中的若干高层主题，以及多个需要未来工作的空白点。

Conclusion: 当前用户级权限处理在AI代理系统中仍存在诸多不足，未来需要在策略表达、推导和执行等方面进一步研究。

Abstract: As AI agents gain prevalance, users are increasingly exposed to the risks such systems entail. Prompt injection attacks, as well as hallucination, can cause agents to leak private information to third parties. As autonomous systems, agents also present the more active danger of performing sensitive tasks, such as bank transactions, without the user's intent or authorization.
  Recognizing this challenge, the agentic security community has developed numerous proposals for secure agentic systems. Much of this work has focused on product-level approaches, where agentic system developers determine and apply the same security policies and permissions to all users. Yet different users have different needs and preferences, necessitating support for user-level permissions policies in agentic AI systems.
  To understand how user-level permissions are handled in AI agent systems, we survey 21 proposals for agent permissions systems. From this review, we construct a taxonomy of how different systems specify user-level permissions policies, both at the user interface and internally; derive internal policies from user input; and enforce those policies at run-time. We then analyze five prominent commercial agents and compare their permissions handling to agentic permissions systems in the literature. We identify several high-level themes across the literature and commerical agents, as well as multiple gaps where future work is needed.

</details>


### [69] [PriEval-Protect: A Unified Framework for Privacy Evaluation and Protection in Healthcare Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13754)
*Ilef Chebil,Asma El Hadj,Souheib Yousfi,Aroua Hedhili,Layth Sliman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PriEval-Protect是一个两阶段框架，通过融合法律合规评分（基于微调法律LLM+RAG）与技术风险评估（加密类型、数据架构、相似性/不确定性/对抗成功率/信息增益损失等指标），使用层次分析法加权聚合得到综合风险评分，并基于评估结果推荐联邦学习、差分隐私等缓解措施，实现医疗隐私保护的法律合规与数据级风险分析的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私合规方法依赖人工、易出错，且将政策审计与数据级评估分离，缺乏统一框架同时处理GDPR/HIPAA合规性与技术层面的隐私风险。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：评估阶段使用微调法律LLM+RAG进行法规合规评分，结合加密类型、数据架构及相似性、不确定性、对抗成功率、信息增益/损失等指标进行技术分析，通过层次分析法加权聚合为综合风险评分；保护阶段根据评估结果推荐联邦学习、差分隐私等对策。

Result: 在医院文档和数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够生成与法规对齐的、可解释的评估结果。

Conclusion: PriEval-Protect成功弥合了法律合规性与数据级风险分析之间的鸿沟，实现了统一的隐私风险评估与缓解。

Abstract: Safeguarding patient privacy while enabling meaningful healthcare data use remains critical under GDPR and HIPAA. Existing compliance methods are manual, error-prone, and separate policy audits from data-level assessments. This paper presents PriEval-Protect, a two-phase framework for unified privacy risk evaluation and mitigation. The evaluation phase combines regulatory compliance scoring using a fine-tuned legal LLM with RAG, and technical analysis via encryption type, data architecture, and metrics including similarity, uncertainty, adversary success, and information gain/loss. A composite risk score uses weighted aggregation via Analytic Hierarchy Process. The protection phase recommends countermeasures including federated learning and differential privacy based on assessed risk. Results on hospital documents and datasets demonstrate regulation-aligned, explainable assessments, bridging legal conformance and data-level risk analysis.

</details>


### [70] [Traffic-Aware Randomized Smoothing for LLM-Based Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13801)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TA-RS，一种针对LLM入侵检测系统的认证防御方法，通过在攻击者可控制的特征子空间注入高斯噪声来提升鲁棒性，在基准数据集上实现了55-100%的认证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的入侵检测系统对流量操纵的鲁棒性研究主要依赖经验性评估，缺乏形式化的鲁棒性保证，且标准随机平滑在LLM-IDS上效果不佳。

Method: 提出TA-RS方法，仅在攻击者可直接控制的特征子空间中注入高斯噪声进行微调和认证，将平滑分布与攻击者可控子空间对齐，并在多个LLM-IDS配置下进行实验验证。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和HIKARI-2021上取得55-100%认证准确率（epsilon=0.05）；中位认证半径超出阈值1.8-5倍；与基线相比残差优势为4-19个百分点（CIC-IDS-2018）；各向同性RS基线因扰动不可控特征导致高达68%的弃权率。

Conclusion: TA-RS通过子空间对齐策略有效提升了LLM-IDS的认证鲁棒性，但方法在RT-IoT2022上存在局限性，需增加噪声增强才能恢复性能，且优势主要来自训练-认证一致性而非单纯的对齐策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are increasingly studied for security monitoring, yet their robustness against feasible traffic manipulation remains largely empirical. We present Traffic-Aware Randomized Smoothing (TA-RS), a classifier-agnostic certified defense that injects Gaussian noise exclusively into the directly controllable (DC) subspace -- features a remote attacker can modify -- during both fine-tuning and certification, aligning the smoothing distribution with the attacker-controllable subspace. We identify a critical prerequisite: applying standard randomized smoothing to clean-trained LLM-IDS yields weak certified accuracy in three of four (model, dataset) pairs tested (14-33%, at or below random) and only 57% in the fourth (43 pp below the noise-augmented result); noise-augmented fine-tuning recovers to 68-100% on two of three benchmark datasets (at sigma=0.25). At the L_inf-equivalent threshold R_inf = epsilon*sqrt(|DC|) (epsilon=0.05), TA-RS achieves 55-100% certified accuracy on CIC-IDS-2018 and HIKARI-2021, with median certified radii (R approx 0.45-0.96) exceeding R_inf by 1.8-5x (across sigma=0.25-1.00). Against a fairly trained iso-trained RS baseline the residual advantage is dataset-dependent (4-19 pp on CIC-IDS-2018). The larger gap -- up to 72 pp against an isotropic RS baseline that shares the DC-noise-augmented training recipe -- primarily reflects the training-certification mismatch rather than DC alignment alone: isotropic test-time noise perturbs uncontrollable features the attacker cannot exploit, triggering abstention rates up to 68%. RT-IoT2022 probes the limits of the method: it fails under the default fine-tuning recipe but recovers to 76%/69% certified accuracy (LLaMA3-8B/Qwen3-8B) when noise augmentation is increased.

</details>


### [71] [Assessing the Forensic Viability of Android Memory Analysis Across Production Builds: A Cross-Version Study of Security Hardening and Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13821)
*Jayasimha Nannapanen,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了Android 8至15系统中libart.so调试符号的剥离程度，发现静态符号从20,495降至零，动态符号下降约60%，源文件引用完全消失。尽管符号被大量剥离，但堆空间、垃圾回收基础设施和分配位图等内存架构在底层仍保持结构完整，可通过开发构建的偏移量在已剥离的生产设备上定位有效指针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Google在消费者手机上大量剥离了libart.so中的调试符号以增强安全性，但以往研究未量化符号剥离的实际进展，也未验证剥离后底层内存架构是否仍与取证文献描述一致。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从Pixel工厂镜像中提取Android 8至15的libart.so二进制文件，统计静态符号、动态符号和源文件引用的变化；通过源码审查和内存映射比较分析Android 8与15的内存架构差异；在完全剥离的Pixel 7设备上进行实时验证。

Result: 静态符号从20,495降至零，动态符号下降约60%，源文件引用完全消失。Android 15新增的压缩回退段恢复了数千个函数名但未包含结构布局。堆空间、GC基础设施和分配位图在底层保持结构完整，变化仅限于命名和算术优化。实时验证表明运行时入口点仍可通过动态符号表定位，开发构建的结构偏移量可解析为生产内存中的有效指针。

Conclusion: 尽管调试符号大幅剥离，Android运行时内存架构的核心结构并未改变，现有的内存取证方法在适度调整后仍可适用于生产设备。

Abstract: Android memory forensics recovers evidence that never touches disk: decrypted messages, session credentials, and the live internal state of a running application. The tools that perform this recovery depend on debug symbols embedded in libart.so, the Android Runtime library, to locate data structures and interpret their layout. Across recent releases Google has stripped most of that information from the binaries that ship on consumer phones as part of a broader security hardening effort, yet no prior work has measured how far the stripping has progressed on the devices examiners actually encounter, or whether the memory architecture beneath the stripped surface still resembles what the forensics literature describes. This paper measures the gap between the unstripped development builds researchers use and the production builds that ship on real hardware, using binaries extracted from Pixel factory images across Android 8 through Android 15. Static symbols fell from 20,495 entries to zero, dynamic symbols dropped by roughly 60 percent, and source file references disappeared entirely. A compressed fallback section in the Android 15 binary restores thousands of function names but carries no structure layouts. Source code review and memory map comparisons across Android 8 and 15 show that the heap spaces, garbage collector infrastructure, and allocation bitmaps remain structurally intact, with visible changes limited to naming and arithmetic optimization. Live validation on a rooted, fully stripped Pixel 7 confirms that the runtime entry point is still locatable through the dynamic symbol table, and that structural offsets pulled from a version-matched development build resolve to valid pointers inside production memory.

</details>


### [72] [Reveal, Correct, Then Pay: Encrypted Mempools and Perpetual Funding Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13832)
*Benjamin Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究加密内存池中自撰状态操纵攻击，以永续合约资金费率为典型例子，揭示隐私保护造成的经济反应缺口，并量化了交易隐私对MEV的多重放大效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密内存池虽能隐藏交易内容以防止受害者依赖型MEV，但攻击者仍可利用自撰状态操纵（如永续合约资金费率）获利。本文旨在研究此类攻击的微观基础与放大机制。

Method: 构建理论模型，将纠错行为建模为可执行的套利机会；纠错者根据局部价格冲击和库存成本选择订单规模；协议信息排期决定哪些机会可执行；引入排序屏障区分封闭阶段与自适应阶段。

Result: 排序屏障使得封闭阶段的纠错率低于自适应纠错率，且进入资金窗口的扭曲被响应因子放大；交易隐私还会降低可预测资金进入价格的资本化程度，产生第二个放大渠道。最终形成由攻击者盲目度、纠错屏蔽度和资本化屏蔽度构成的安全指数。

Conclusion: 交易隐私在消除某些类型MEV的同时，可能通过自撰状态操纵引入新的经济安全漏洞，其影响可通过结构化安全指数进行度量和缓解。

Abstract: Encrypted mempools are designed to hide transaction contents until execution order is fixed, preventing many victim dependent forms of maximal extractable value. This paper studies a different class of attack in the form of self-authored state manipulation, in which the attacker knows its own transaction and owns a downstream claim on the state that transaction changes. Perpetual futures funding is a canonical example. The funding signal determines a transfer rate, while receiving side open interest is the transfer base. In a commit then reveal mempool, an adaptive corrective transaction cannot enter the already committed batch. Privacy can therefore create an economic reaction gap even when cryptographic decryption overhead is negligible. We microfound correction through executable arbitrage opportunities. Correctors choose order size against local price impact and inventory cost, while the protocol information schedule determines which opportunities are actionable. The ordering barrier removes ordinary adaptive searchers from the closed stage. It therefore yields a closed stage correction rate below the adaptive correction rate whenever positive adaptive capacity becomes available after reveal. The distortion entering a funding window is multiplied by an explicit response factor. Transaction privacy can also reduce capitalization of predictable funding into entry prices, producing a second amplification channel. The resulting local security index separates attacker blindness, correction shielding, and capitalization shielding.

</details>


### [73] [From Classification to Consistent Templates: Multiple Permuted-Label Classifier Encoding for Biometric Template Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13845)
*Baogang Song,Zhongshu Zhao,Qianrong Zheng,Jianwen Xiang,Dongdong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出多置换标签分类器编码（MPLCE），通过分类器特定标签置换和哈希实现不可逆、可撤销、不可链接的生物特征模板保护，在多个面部和虹膜数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物特征模板保护方法存在相似性结构可被利用、辅助数据风险、依赖人工目标或与特定模态耦合等问题。基于哈希的精确匹配需要生成相同中间模板，身份分类天然满足此要求，因此利用分类器输出实现保护。

Method: 对每个分类器进行特定标签置换，为同一身份在不同分类器中分配不同标签；编码并拼接预测标签形成中间模板；应用特定XOR字符串随机化后哈希，实现精确匹配验证，无需纠错码或辅助数据。

Result: 在四个面部和两个虹膜数据集上取得竞争性能：YTF上GAR 98.61%（FAR 5.51×10⁻⁵%），CASIA-Iris-Lamp上GAR 99.10%（FAR 0.00%）。安全性分析支持其不可逆性、可撤销性和不可链接性。

Conclusion: MPLCE通过多分类器标签置换编码与哈希结合，实现了跨模态通用、无需辅助数据、具有强安全属性的生物特征模板保护方案。

Abstract: Biometric template protection (BTP) must secure stored templates while tolerating intra-class variations. Existing methods rely on protected-domain similarity matching, error correction, or predefined-template mappings, potentially retaining exploitable similarity structures, introducing helper-data risks, depending on artificial targets, or coupling protection to specific modalities. Storing only cryptographic hash digests eliminates directly comparable representations and conceals pre-hash templates, but hash-based exact-match verification requires genuine samples to generate identical intermediate templates before hashing. Identity classification is naturally suited to this requirement because it maps variable biometric samples to stable and discriminative identity-level outputs. Based on this insight, we propose Multiple Permuted-Label Classifier Encoding (MPLCE). Through classifier-specific label permutations, MPLCE assigns each identity different labels across multiple classifiers. The predicted labels are encoded and concatenated to form an intermediate template, preventing repeated encodings of a single identity label and enlarging the effective candidate space while preserving classification consistency. The template is randomized with an application-specific XOR string and cryptographically hashed, enabling exact-match verification without error correction codes or biometric-dependent helper data. Using modality-specific classifiers, MPLCE retains the same template generation and protection procedure across modalities. On four face and two iris datasets, MPLCE achieves competitive performance, including a GAR of 98.61\% at a FAR of 5.51\(\times\)10\textsuperscript{-5}\% on YTF and a GAR of 99.10\% at a FAR of 0.00\% on CASIA-Iris-Lamp. Security analyses and attack evaluations support its irreversibility, revocability, and unlinkability under the threat model.

</details>


### [74] [Plausible Deniability Guarantees for Whistleblowers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13928)
*Leo Richter,Matt J. Kusner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了举报人保护问题，将其建模为审计选择过程中的（0, δ）-差分隐私，并证明随机响应方法无法超越均匀随机审计。作者提出了一种通用机制，将私有审计归约到持续计数问题，实现了噪声为O(√log T)的隐私保护，并在模拟中展示了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 举报人面临报复威胁不愿举报，现有保护方案缺乏形式化隐私保证，且差分隐私方法未针对自然威胁模型（被审计组织观察审计选择以识别举报人）进行设计。

Method: 将审计选择形式化为每报告（0, δ）-差分隐私保护；证明随机响应方法性能受限于均匀随机审计；提出将私有审计归约到持续计数的通用机制，利用现有持续计数工作实现O(√log T)噪声；推导选择误差消失的理论条件。

Result: 随机响应方法无法超越均匀随机审计超过δ；所提机制实现每报告（0, δ）-差分隐私，噪声为O(√log T)；当最受举报组织与第二名之间的噪声报告差距增长快于√log T时，选择误差消失；模拟显示显著优于随机响应。

Conclusion: 提供了首个针对审计场景强敌手模型的正式隐私保证框架，并给出了可实现的实用机制。

Abstract: Whistleblowers are a key safeguard against organizational wrongdoing, but the threat of retaliation deters reporting. Existing whistleblower-protection proposals lack formal privacy guarantees, and existing differential privacy mechanisms do not directly target the natural threat model -- one in which the audited organization itself observes auditor selection decisions and uses them to identify reporters. We formalize protection against a strong-adversary threat model as per-report $(0, δ)$-differential privacy on the transcript of audit selections. Within this framework we prove that a natural approach -- randomized response applied at the selection step -- can never outperform uniform random auditing by more than $δ$ at any horizon. We then give a generic mechanism that reduces private auditing to private continual counting: any $(0, δ)$-DP continual counter plugs in by post-processing, and the audit transcript inherits the same per-report guarantee. Instantiating the reduction with a recent work in continual counting yields per-report $(0, δ)$-DP with noise scaling as $O(\sqrt{\log T})$ across a horizon of $T$ audit decisions. A utility theorem shows that the selection error vanishes whenever the noisy report gap between the most-reported organization and the runner-up grows faster than $\sqrt{\log T}$. Simulations show a substantial improvement over randomized response.

</details>


### [75] [HORCRUX: A Complete PQC RISC-V eXtension Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13939)
*Alessandra Dolmeta,Valeria Piscopo,Michael Hutter,Maurizio Martina,Guido Masera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HORCRUX 是一种紧凑的 RISC-V 扩展，通过统一指令集支持所有 NIST 批准的 PQC 算法，在资源受限的嵌入式系统中实现了密码敏捷性、高性能和低资源消耗的平衡，在 FPGA 上实现了高达 129 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬件扩展通常只支持有限的 PQC 子集，难以在密码敏捷性、高性能和低资源消耗之间取得平衡，尤其缺乏针对资源受限嵌入式系统的统一解决方案。

Method: 通过分析 ML-KEM、ML-DSA、SLH-DSA、HQC 和 Falcon 的共享内核，设计统一的指令集扩展，由资源高效的紧耦合协处理器执行，采用模块化架构保持与标准 RISC-V 核的向后兼容性。

Result: 在 Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA 上实现哈希方案 129 倍、格基方案 9 倍、编码基方案 27 倍的加速，资源开销少于 21k LUTs 和 4.4k FFs；在 65 nm CMOS 下进行了 ASIC 后综合表征和功耗验证。

Conclusion: HORCRUX 为资源受限的嵌入式系统提供了可扩展的 PQC 部署方案，在保持向后兼容性的同时实现了显著的性能加速和能源效率。

Abstract: This work presents a compact RISC-V extension for Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) called HORCRUX, which provides a unified Instruction-Set Extension (ISE) supporting all NIST-approved PQC algorithms. HORCRUX addresses the difficult trade-off between crypto-agility, high performance, and low resource consumption in constrained environments, a balance typically missing in hardware extensions that focus on limited PQC subsets. By targeting shared kernels across ML-KEM, MLDSA, SLH-DSA, HQC, and Falcon, the extension introduces new RISC-V instructions executed by a resource-efficient, tightly coupled coprocessor. This architecture is specifically optimized for embedded systems with strict energy budgets and limited area. Experimental evaluation on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA demonstrates speedups of up to 129x for hash-based, 9x for lattice-based, and 27x for code-based schemes, while adding fewer than 21k LUTs and 4.4k FFs. ASIC results from postsynthesis characterization in 65 nm CMOS are also reported, alongside a rigorous power characterization to validate the architecture's energy efficiency. The extension's modular structure maintains backward compatibility with standard RISC-V cores, offering a scalable solution for deploying PQC on constrained embedded systems.

</details>


### [76] [Agent Skill Security: Threat Models, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13987)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSec-Eval框架，用于系统性评估可复用LLM agent技能在整个生命周期中的安全性，通过327个真实技能的实证研究，发现漏洞不仅存在于执行阶段，还广泛分布于仓库准入、语义检索、规划器选择和技能演化等多个生命周期阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全研究主要集中在提示注入和运行时执行，而对可复用技能在整个生命周期中的安全风险缺乏系统性探索。

Method: 首先刻画技能生命周期，构建覆盖仓库准入、语义检索、规划器选择、执行和技能演化五个阶段的威胁分类体系；然后基于该分类体系实例化SkillSec-Eval框架，并在包含327个真实技能的仓库上进行综合实证评估。

Result: 研究发现漏洞在多个生命周期阶段（超出执行阶段）出现，表明需要对可复用agent技能进行全生命周期的安全分析。

Conclusion: 可复用技能的安全性不能仅关注执行阶段，需要建立生命周期感知的安全分析框架。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a fundamental building block of Large Language Model (LLM) agents, enabling capabilities to be packaged, shared, and reused across diverse applications. However, existing security research primarily focuses on prompt injection and runtime execution, leaving security risks throughout the broader skill lifecycle largely unexplored. In this paper, we present SkillSec-Eval, a lifecycle-aware framework for systematically evaluating the security of reusable agent skills. We first characterize the skill lifecycle and develop a threat taxonomy spanning repository admission, semantic retrieval, planner selection, execution, and skill evolution. We then instantiate this taxonomy in SkillSec-Eval and conduct a comprehensive empirical evaluation using a repository of 327 real-world skills. Our study demonstrates that vulnerabilities arise at multiple lifecycle stages beyond execution, highlighting the need for lifecycle-aware security analysis of reusable agent skills.

</details>


### [77] [Rethinking Penetration Testing for AI-Enabled Systems: From Resource Compromise to Behavioral Objective Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14006)
*Mohammad Allahbakhsh,Mohammad Hassan Bahari,Moslem Attar-Raouf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了AI驱动系统的渗透测试：从传统的基础设施攻防转向以目标为导向的行为评估，提出包含6个步骤的测试工作流，并定义了AI渗透为通过提示注入、数据投毒等行为路径诱导AI违反操作目标的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试仅关注基础设施漏洞，但对于AI系统，攻击者可以通过影响提示、训练数据、工具等行为层面改变系统输出，而不需要直接攻破底层基础设施。因此需要一种新的评估框架来覆盖这些攻击面。

Method: 提出了一个包含6个步骤的行为导向渗透测试工作流：(1)识别操作目标，(2)映射AI控制行为，(3)分析对抗性影响面，(4)定义行为失效标准，(5)执行场景化测试，(6)报告攻击行动与目标违反之间的证据链。

Result: 通过一个AI驱动的安全运营中心(SOC)助手的案例，展示了渗透可能通过行为影响（而非基础设施入侵）发生，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 重新定义的AI渗透测试框架能够覆盖提示注入、间接提示注入、数据投毒、传感器操纵、检索投毒、工具误用和智能体失调等新型攻击路径，为已部署AI系统的对抗性成败评估提供了技术框架。

Abstract: Penetration testing traditionally evaluates whether adversaries can exploit weaknesses in software, infrastructure, configurations, or operational controls to achieve security-relevant compromise. This paradigm remains necessary for AI-enabled systems, but it is no longer sufficient. In such systems, adversaries may influence prompts, retrieved content, sensor inputs, training data, memory, tools, or human-AI interaction loops to alter system behavior without directly compromising the underlying infrastructure. This paper reframes penetration testing for AI-enabled systems as objective-driven behavioral evaluation. We define an AI-enabled system as one in which learned models materially influence behavior affecting operational outcomes, and we define AI-enabled penetration as the feasible induction of AI-governed behavior that violates one or more operational objectives under an explicit threat model. This definition preserves conventional penetration testing while extending it to adversarial pathways such as prompt injection, indirect prompt injection, data poisoning, sensor manipulation, retrieval poisoning, tool misuse, and agentic misalignment. We further propose a testing workflow that identifies operational objectives, maps AI-governed behavior, analyzes adversarial influence surfaces, defines behavioral failure criteria, executes scenario-based tests, and reports evidence linking adversarial action to objective violation. A running example involving an AI-enabled security operations center assistant illustrates how penetration may occur through behavioral influence rather than infrastructure compromise. Together, the definitions, workflow, and example provide a technical framework for evaluating adversarial success in deployed AI-enabled systems.

</details>
